Mock theta functions and indefinite modular forms II
Minoru Wakimoto

Abstract




Abstract

In this paper, we compute the Zwegers’s modification of the mock theta
functions
\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}
and study the modular transformation
properties of the indefinite modular forms which appear in the explicit
formula for the modified functions
\widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}
.

1
Introduction

In the previous paper
[
17
]
we studied indefinite modular forms
obtained from the Zwegers’ modification of the mock theta function

\Phi^{(-)[m,\frac{1}{2}]}
for
m\in\frac{1}{2}{\mathbf{N}}_{\rm odd}
.
In the current paper, we study the case
m\in{\mathbf{N}}
where,
in order that the Zwegers’ modification works, we consider
the function

\Phi^{(\pm)[m,s]\,\ast}\,\ :=\,\ \Phi^{(\pm)[m,s]}_{1}+\Phi^{(\pm)[m,s]}_{2}

(1.1)

with
\Phi^{(\pm)[m,s]}_{i}

(i=1,2)
defined in
[
17
]
.

Comparing the results in the current paper with those in the previous
paper
[
17
]
, there appear strange difference
between the case
m\in{\mathbf{N}}
and
the case
m\in\frac{1}{2}{\mathbf{N}}_{\rm odd}
.
In the case
m\in\frac{1}{2}{\mathbf{N}}_{\rm odd}
in
[
17
]
,
we need 3 series of functions
g^{(i)[m,p]}_{k}(\tau)

(i=1,2,3)
to get
SL_{2}({\mathbf{Z}})
-invariant family for each

m\in\frac{1}{2}{\mathbf{N}}_{\rm odd}
whereas, in the case

m\in{\mathbf{N}}
in the current paper, only one series of functions

g^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau)
span
SL_{2}({\mathbf{Z}})
-invariant spaces
for each
m\in{\mathbf{N}}
.

This paper is organized as follows.

In section
2
, we make preparation on basic properties
of
\Phi^{(\pm)[m,s]\,\ast}
.
In section
3
, we derive the explicit formula for

\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)
by using the Kac-Peterson’s
identity just in the similar way with
[
19
]
.
In section
4
, we deduce the formula
for
\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1}+p\tau,z_{2}-p\tau,0)
in the case
when

(z_{1},z_{2})=\Big(\frac{z}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}-\frac{1}{2},\,\frac{z}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\Big)\hskip 14.22636pt{\rm and}\hskip 14.22636pt(z_{1},z_{2})=\Big(\frac{z}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2},\,\frac{z}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}\Big).

(1.2)

In section
5
, we compute the
Zwegers’s correction function
\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}_{\rm add}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)

for
(z_{1},z_{2})
given by (
1.2
).
In section
6
, using the
results obtained in §
5
,
we make detail investigation on the relation between

\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1}+p\tau,z_{2}-p\tau,0)
and
the modified function

\widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1}+p\tau,z_{2}-p\tau,0)

when
(z_{1},z_{2})
is given by (
1.2
).
In section
7
, we obtain the explicit formula
for
\widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2}0)

in the case when
(z_{1},z_{2})
is given by (
1.2
).
In section
8
, we introduce functions

\Xi^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)
and
\Upsilon^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

for
p\in{\mathbf{Z}}
such that
0\leq p\leq 2m
and
i\in\{1,2\}
,
and compute the modular transformation properties of these functions.
In section
9
, we define the functions

G^{(i)[m,p]\ast}(\tau,z)
and
g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau)
by

\displaystyle G^{(i)[m,p]\ast}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\Xi^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)-\Upsilon^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,m\end{subarray}}g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau)[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}](\tau,z)

and compute modular transformation of these functions.
In section
10
, using the relation between

g^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau)
and
g^{(2)[m,p]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau)
,
we define the indefinite modular forms
g^{[m,p]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau)

and obtain their modular transformation properties.

2
Preliminaries

Lemma 2.1
.

Let
m\in\frac{1}{2}{\mathbf{N}}
,
s\in\frac{1}{2}{\mathbf{Z}}
and
a\in{\mathbf{Z}}
. Then

\Phi^{(\pm)[m,s]\ast}(\tau,z_{1}+a\tau,z_{2}-a\tau,t)=(\pm 1)^{a}\,e^{2\pi ima(z_{1}-z_{2})}q^{ma^{2}}\Phi^{(\pm)[m,s-2am]\ast}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},t)

(2.1)

Proof.

This follows immediately from Lemma 2.3 in
[
17
]
and
definition of
\Phi^{(\pm)[m,s]\ast}
.
∎

Lemma 2.2
.

Let
m\in\frac{1}{2}{\mathbf{N}}
,
s\in\frac{1}{2}{\mathbf{Z}}
and

a\in{\mathbf{Z}}_{\geq 0}
. Then

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt\Phi^{(\pm)[m,s]\,\ast}(\tau,\,z_{1}+a\tau,\,z_{2}-a\tau,\,0)

(2.2)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\pm 1)^{a}e^{2\pi ima(z_{1}-z_{2})}q^{ma^{2}}\,\bigg\{\Phi^{(\pm)[m,s]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
1\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,2am\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581pte^{-\pi i(k-s)(z_{1}-z_{2})}\,q^{-\frac{1}{4m}(k-s)^{2}}\,\big[\theta_{k-s,m}^{(\pm)}+\theta_{-(k-s),m}^{(\pm)}\big](\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})\bigg\}

Proof.

This lemma can be shown in the similar way with the
proof of Lemma 2.4 in
[
17
]
as folloes.
By Lemma 2.1 in
[
16
]
with Remark 2.1 in
[
17
]
,
we have

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt\Phi^{(\pm)[m,s-a]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)\,\ -\,\ \Phi^{(\pm)[m,s]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-a\leq k\leq-1\end{subarray}}e^{\pi i(s+k)(z_{1}-z_{2})}\,q^{-\frac{(s+k)^{2}}{4m}}\,\big[\theta^{(\pm)}_{s+k,\,m}+\theta^{(\pm)}_{-(s+k),\,m}\big](\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})

Letting
a\rightarrow 2am
, we have

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt\Phi^{(\pm)[m,s-2am]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)\,-\,\Phi^{(\pm)[m,s]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)

(2.3)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-2am\leq k\leq-1\end{subarray}}e^{\pi i(s+k)(z_{1}-z_{2})}q^{-\frac{(s+k)^{2}}{4m}}\big[\theta^{(\pm)}_{s+k,\,m}+\theta^{(\pm)}_{-(s+k),\,m}\big](\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt\underset{\begin{subarray}{c}\\[1.42262pt]
\uparrow\\[2.84526pt]
k\,\rightarrow\,-k\end{subarray}}{=}\hskip-14.22636pt

\displaystyle\,\ \sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
1\leq k\leq 2am\end{subarray}}e^{\pi i(s-k)(z_{1}-z_{2})}\,q^{-\frac{(s-k)^{2}}{4m}}\big[\theta^{(\pm)}_{k-s,\,m}+\theta^{(\pm)}_{-(k-s),\,m}\big](\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})

Substituting this equation (
2.3
) into
(
2.1
), we obtain (
2.2
),
proving Lemma
2.2
.
∎

Lemma 2.3
.

Let
m\in\frac{1}{2}{\mathbf{N}}
,
s\in\frac{1}{2}{\mathbf{Z}}
and
p\in{\mathbf{Z}}

such that
mp\in{\mathbf{Z}}
. Then

1)

if
p\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt\Phi^{(\pm)[m,s]\,\ast}(\tau,\,z_{1},\,z_{2}+p\tau,\,0)\,\ =\,\ e^{-2\pi impz_{1}}\,\Phi^{(\pm)[m,s]\,\ast}(\tau,\,z_{1},\,z_{2},\,0)

\displaystyle-\,\ e^{-2\pi impz_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{mp-1}e^{\pi i(s+k)(z_{1}-z_{2})}q^{-\frac{(s+k)^{2}}{4m}}\,\big[\theta^{(\pm)}_{s+k,m}+\theta^{(\pm)}_{-(s+k),m}\big](\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})

2)

if
p<0
,

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt\Phi^{(\pm)[m,s]\,\ast}(\tau,\,z_{1},\,z_{2}+p\tau,\,0)\,\ =\,\ e^{-2\pi impz_{1}}\,\Phi^{(\pm)[m,s]\,\ast}(\tau,\,z_{1},\,z_{2},\,0)

\displaystyle+\,\ e^{-2\pi impz_{1}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
mp\,\leq\,k\,<\,0\end{subarray}}e^{\pi i(s+k)(z_{1}-z_{2})}q^{-\frac{(s+k)^{2}}{4m}}\,\big[\theta^{(\pm)}_{s+k,m}+\theta^{(\pm)}_{-(s+k),m}\big](\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})

Proof.

These formulas follow immediately from Lemma 2.5 in
[
16
]
and
definition of
\Phi^{(\pm)[m,s]\ast}
.
∎

Following formulas for theta functions can be shown easily by using
Lemma 1.1 in
[
17
]
and Note 1.1 in
[
17
]
,
and will be used in the proof of Lemmas
4.1

and
4.2
.

Note 2.1
.

For
m\in{\mathbf{N}}
and
p\in{\mathbf{Z}}
, the following formulas hold:

1)

\theta_{\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}\Big(\tau,\,\dfrac{m(2p+1)\tau-m}{m+\frac{1}{2}}\Big)\,\ =\,\ e^{-\frac{\pi im}{2m+1}}\,q^{-\frac{m^{2}}{2(2m+1)}(2p+1)^{2}}\,\theta_{2mp+(m+\frac{1}{2}),\,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)

2)

\theta_{\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}\Big(\tau,\,\dfrac{m(2p+1)\tau}{m+\frac{1}{2}}\Big)\,\ =\,\ q^{-\frac{m^{2}}{2(2m+1)}(2p+1)^{2}}\,\theta_{2mp+(m+\frac{1}{2}),\,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)

Note 2.2
.

For
m\in\frac{1}{2}{\mathbf{N}}
and
p\in{\mathbf{Z}}
, the following formulas hold:

1)

(i)

\theta_{-\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}\Big(\tau,\,\ z+\dfrac{(2p+1)\tau-1}{2m+1}\Big)\,=\,\ e^{\frac{\pi i}{2(2m+1)}}\,q^{-\frac{1}{16(m+\frac{1}{2})}(2p+1)^{2}}e^{-\frac{\pi i}{2}(2p+1)z}\,\theta_{p,m+\frac{1}{2}}(\tau,z)

(ii)

\theta_{\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}\Big(\tau,\,\ z-\dfrac{(2p+1)\tau-1}{2m+1}\Big)\,=\,\ e^{\frac{\pi i}{2(2m+1)}}\,q^{-\frac{1}{16(m+\frac{1}{2})}(2p+1)^{2}}e^{\frac{\pi i}{2}(2p+1)z}\,\theta_{-p,m+\frac{1}{2}}(\tau,z)

2)

(i)

\theta_{-\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}\Big(\tau,\,\ z+\dfrac{(2p+1)\tau}{2m+1}\Big)\,=\,\ q^{-\frac{1}{16(m+\frac{1}{2})}(2p+1)^{2}}\,e^{-\frac{\pi i}{2}(2p+1)z}\,\theta_{p,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,z)

(ii)

\theta_{\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}\Big(\tau,\,\ z-\dfrac{(2p+1)\tau}{2m+1}\Big)\,=\,\ q^{-\frac{1}{16(m+\frac{1}{2})}(2p+1)^{2}}\,e^{\frac{\pi i}{2}(2p+1)z}\,\theta_{-p,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,z)

Note 2.3
.

For
p\in{\mathbf{Z}}
, the following formulas hold:

1)

(i)

\vartheta_{11}\Big(\tau,\,\dfrac{z}{2}+\dfrac{(2p+1)\tau-1}{2}\Big)\,\ =\,\ q^{-\frac{1}{8}(2p+1)^{2}}\,e^{-\frac{\pi i}{2}(2p+1)z}\,\theta_{0,\frac{1}{2}}(\tau,z)

(ii)

\vartheta_{11}\Big(\tau,\,\dfrac{z}{2}-\dfrac{(2p+1)\tau-1}{2}\Big)\,\ =\,\ -\,q^{-\frac{1}{8}(2p+1)^{2}}\,e^{\frac{\pi i}{2}(2p+1)z}\,\theta_{0,\frac{1}{2}}(\tau,z)

2)

(i)

\vartheta_{11}\Big(\tau,\,\ \dfrac{z}{2}+\Big(p+\dfrac{1}{2}\Big)\tau\Big)\,\ =\,\ -\,i\,(-1)^{p}\,q^{-\frac{1}{8}(2p+1)^{2}}\,e^{-\frac{\pi i}{2}(2p+1)z}\,\theta_{0,\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,z)

(ii)

\vartheta_{11}\Big(\tau,\,\ \dfrac{z}{2}-\Big(p+\dfrac{1}{2}\Big)\tau\Big)\,\ =\,\ i\,(-1)^{p}\,q^{-\frac{1}{8}(2p+1)^{2}}\,e^{\frac{\pi i}{2}(2p+1)z}\,\theta_{0,\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,z)

3
Explicit formula for
\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)

Proposition 3.1
.

For
m\in\frac{1}{2}{\mathbf{N}}
,

\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)
is given by the
following formula:

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt\theta_{\frac{1}{2},\,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}\Big(\tau,\,\ \frac{m(z_{1}-z_{2})}{m+\frac{1}{2}}\Big)\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\ \bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,k\,\leq\,2mj\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
2mj\,<\,k\,\leq\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg]\,(-1)^{j}q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j+\frac{1}{4(m+\frac{1}{2})})^{2}}q^{-\frac{k^{2}}{4m}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\,\ e^{2\pi im(j+\frac{1}{4(m+\frac{1}{2})})(z_{1}-z_{2})}\,e^{-\pi ik(z_{1}-z_{2})}\,\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})\hskip 28.45274pt

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt+\,\ i\,\eta(\tau)^{3}\Bigg\{-\,\frac{\displaystyle\theta_{-\frac{1}{2},\,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}\Big(\tau,\,\ z_{1}+z_{2}+\frac{z_{1}-z_{2}}{2m+1}\Big)}{\vartheta_{11}(\tau,z_{1})}+\frac{\displaystyle\theta_{\frac{1}{2},\,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}\Big(\tau,\,\ z_{1}+z_{2}-\frac{z_{1}-z_{2}}{2m+1}\Big)}{\vartheta_{11}(\tau,z_{2})}\Bigg\}

Proof.

Lettig
z_{1}=z_{2}
in the formulas (3.1a) and (3.1b)
in
[
18
]
and making their sum, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in{\mathbf{Z}}}(-1)^{j}q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})j^{2}+\frac{1}{2}j}\,e^{-2\pi ijm(z_{1}-z_{3})}\,\big[\underbrace{\Phi^{[m,0]}_{1}+\Phi^{[m,0]}_{2}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[0.0pt]
{\displaystyle\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}}\end{subarray}}\big](\tau,\,z_{1},\,-z_{3}-2j\tau,\,0)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{-\pi iz_{1}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbf{Z}}}(-1)^{k}q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})k^{2}-\frac{1}{2}k}\,e^{2\pi ikm(z_{1}-z_{3})}\,\Phi^{(-)[\frac{1}{2},\frac{1}{2}]}_{1}(\tau,\,z_{1},\,-z_{1}-2k\tau,\,0)

\displaystyle\hskip-12.80373pt+\,\ e^{-\pi iz_{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbf{Z}}}(-1)^{k}q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})k^{2}-\frac{1}{2}k}\,e^{-2\pi ikm(z_{1}-z_{3})}\,\Phi^{(-)[\frac{1}{2},\frac{1}{2}]}_{1}(\tau,\,z_{3},\,-z_{3}-2k\tau,\,0)

Letting
(j,k)\rightarrow(-j,-k)
and changing the notation

-z_{3}\rightarrow z_{2}
, this formula becomes:

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in{\mathbf{Z}}}(-1)^{j}q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})j^{2}-\frac{1}{2}j}\,e^{2\pi ijm(z_{1}+z_{2})}\,\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,\,z_{1},\,z_{2}+2j\tau,\,0)

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt=\,\ e^{-\pi iz_{1}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbf{Z}}}(-1)^{k}q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})k^{2}+\frac{1}{2}k}\,e^{-2\pi ikm(z_{1}+z_{2})}\,\Phi^{(-)[\frac{1}{2},\frac{1}{2}]}_{1}(\tau,\,z_{1},\,-z_{1}+2k\tau,\,0)

\displaystyle\hskip-25.60747pt+\,\ e^{\pi iz_{2}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbf{Z}}}(-1)^{k}q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})k^{2}+\frac{1}{2}k}\,e^{2\pi ikm(z_{1}+z_{2})}\,\Phi^{(-)[\frac{1}{2},\frac{1}{2}]}_{1}(\tau,\,-z_{2},\,z_{2}+2k\tau,\,0)

(3.2)

First we compute the LHS of this equation (
3.2
):

LHS of (
3.2
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{q^{-\frac{1}{16(m+\frac{1}{2})}}\sum_{j\in{\mathbf{Z}}_{\geq 0}}(-1)^{j}q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j-\frac{1}{4(m+\frac{1}{2})})^{2}}e^{2\pi ijm(z_{1}+z_{2})}\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,\,z_{1},\,z_{2}+2j\tau,\,0)}_{\rm(I)}

\displaystyle+\,\underbrace{q^{-\frac{1}{16(m+\frac{1}{2})}}\sum_{j\in{\mathbf{Z}}_{<0}}(-1)^{j}q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j-\frac{1}{4(m+\frac{1}{2})})^{2}}e^{2\pi ijm(z_{1}+z_{2})}\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,\,z_{1},\,z_{2}+2j\tau,\,0)}_{\rm(II)}

where (I) and (II) are computed by using Lemma
2.3
as folloows:

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt{\rm(I)}\,=\,q^{-\frac{1}{16(m+\frac{1}{2})}}\sum_{j\in{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm\geq 0}}(-1)^{j}q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j-\frac{1}{4(m+\frac{1}{2})})^{2}}e^{-2\pi ijm(z_{1}-z_{2})}\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ q^{-\frac{1}{16(m+\frac{1}{2})}}\sum_{j\in{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm\geq 0}}\sum_{k=0}^{2mj-1}(-1)^{j}q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j-\frac{1}{4(m+\frac{1}{2})})^{2}}\hskip-2.84526ptq^{-\frac{k^{2}}{4m}}e^{-2\pi ijm(z_{1}-z_{2})}e^{\pi ik(z_{1}-z_{2})}

\displaystyle\times\,\ \big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt{\rm(II)}\,=\,q^{-\frac{1}{16(m+\frac{1}{2})}}\sum_{j\in{\mathbf{Z}}_{<0}}(-1)^{j}q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j-\frac{1}{4(m+\frac{1}{2})})^{2}}e^{-2\pi ijm(z_{1}-z_{2})}\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)

\displaystyle\hskip-22.76219pt+\,\ q^{-\frac{1}{16(m+\frac{1}{2})}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
2mj\,\leq\,k\,<\,0\end{subarray}}(-1)^{j}q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j-\frac{1}{4(m+\frac{1}{2})})^{2}}\hskip-2.84526ptq^{-\frac{k^{2}}{4m}}e^{-2\pi ijm(z_{1}-z_{2})}e^{\pi ik(z_{1}-z_{2})}

\displaystyle\times\,\ \big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})

Then we have

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt\text{LHS of \eqref{m1:eqn:2022-917e}}\,\ =\,\ {\rm(I)}+{\rm(II)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle q^{-\frac{1}{16(m+\frac{1}{2})}}\underbrace{\sum_{j\in{\mathbf{Z}}}(-1)^{j}q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j-\frac{1}{4(m+\frac{1}{2})})^{2}}e^{-2\pi ijm(z_{1}-z_{2})}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[0.0pt]
{\displaystyle e^{\frac{-\pi im}{2m+1}(z_{1}-z_{2})}\,\theta_{\frac{1}{2},\,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}\Big(\tau,\,\ \frac{m}{m+\frac{1}{2}}(z_{1}-z_{2})\Big)}\end{subarray}}\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ q^{-\frac{1}{16(m+\frac{1}{2})}}\bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,<\,2mj\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
2mj\,\leq\,k\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\,\ (-1)^{j}q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j-\frac{1}{4(m+\frac{1}{2})})^{2}}\hskip-2.84526ptq^{-\frac{k^{2}}{4m}}e^{-2\pi ijm(z_{1}-z_{2})}e^{\pi ik(z_{1}-z_{2})}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})

Next we compute the RHS of (
3.2
) by using
Lemma 2.1 in
[
19
]
:

RHS of (
3.2
)

(3.3b)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{-\pi iz_{1}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbf{Z}}}(-1)^{k}q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})k^{2}+\frac{1}{2}k}\,e^{-2\pi ikm(z_{1}+z_{2})}\underbrace{\Phi^{(-)[\frac{1}{2},\frac{1}{2}]}_{1}(\tau,\,z_{1},\,-z_{1}+2k\tau,\,0)}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[-5.69054pt]
{\displaystyle-\,i\,e^{-2\pi ikz_{1}}\,\frac{\eta(\tau)^{3}}{\vartheta_{11}(\tau,z_{1})}}\end{subarray}}

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt+\,\ e^{\pi iz_{2}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbf{Z}}}(-1)^{k}q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})k^{2}+\frac{1}{2}k}\,e^{2\pi ikm(z_{1}+z_{2})}\underbrace{\Phi^{(-)[\frac{1}{2},\frac{1}{2}]}_{1}(\tau,\,-z_{2},\,z_{2}+2k\tau,\,0)}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[-5.69054pt]
{\displaystyle-\,i\,e^{2\pi ikz_{2}}\,\frac{\eta(\tau)^{3}}{\vartheta_{11}(\tau,-z_{2})}}\end{subarray}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\ i\,q^{-\frac{1}{16(m+\frac{1}{2})}}\,e^{-\frac{\pi im}{2(m+\frac{1}{2})}(z_{1}-z_{2})}

\displaystyle\times\,\ \underbrace{\sum_{k\in{\mathbf{Z}}}(-1)^{k}\,q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(k+\frac{1}{4(m+\frac{1}{2})})^{2}}\,e^{-2\pi i(m+\frac{1}{2})(k+\frac{1}{4(m+\frac{1}{2})})\cdot\frac{m(z_{1}+z_{2})+z_{1}}{m+\frac{1}{2}}}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[-5.69054pt]
{\displaystyle\hskip 39.83385pt\theta_{\frac{1}{2},\,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}\Big(\tau,\,\ -\,\frac{m(z_{1}+z_{2})+z_{1}}{m+\frac{1}{2}}\Big)}\end{subarray}}\frac{\eta(\tau)^{3}}{\vartheta_{11}(\tau,z_{1})}

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt+\,\ i\,q^{-\frac{1}{16(m+\frac{1}{2})}}\,e^{-\frac{\pi im}{2(m+\frac{1}{2})}(z_{1}-z_{2})}\,

\displaystyle\times\,\ q^{-\frac{(2mr+s)^{2}}{4m}-\frac{1}{2}(2mr+s)(2p+1)}\,\big[\theta_{s,m}+\theta_{-s,m}\big](\tau,z)

The LHS of this equation (

Also (I) is computed by using Notes

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91692pt{\rm(I)}\,=\,-\,\frac{\displaystyle\theta_{-\frac{1}{2},\,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}\Big(\tau,\,\ z+\frac{(2p+1)\tau-1}{2m+1}\Big)}{\displaystyle\vartheta_{11}\Big(\tau,\,\frac{z}{2}+\frac{(2p+1)\tau-1}{2}\Big)}\,+\,\frac{\displaystyle\theta_{\frac{1}{2},\,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}\Big(\tau,\,\ z-\frac{(2p+1)\tau-1}{2m+1}\Big)}{\displaystyle\vartheta_{11}\Big(\tau,\,\frac{z}{2}-\frac{(2p+1)\tau-1}{2}\Big)}

\displaystyle-\,\ \frac{e^{\frac{\pi i}{2(2m+1)}}\,q^{-\frac{1}{16(m+\frac{1}{2})}(2p+1)^{2}}e^{-\frac{\pi i}{2}(2p+1)z}\theta_{p,m+\frac{1}{2}}(\tau,0)}{q^{-\frac{1}{8}(2p+1)^{2}}e^{-\frac{\pi i}{2}(2p+1)z}\theta_{0,\frac{1}{2}}(\tau,z)}

\displaystyle+\,\ \frac{e^{\frac{\pi i}{2(2m+1)}}\,q^{-\frac{1}{16(m+\frac{1}{2})}(2p+1)^{2}}e^{\frac{\pi i}{2}(2p+1)z}\theta_{-p,m+\frac{1}{2}}(\tau,0)}{-\,q^{-\frac{1}{8}(2p+1)^{2}}e^{\frac{\pi i}{2}(2p+1)z}\theta_{0,\frac{1}{2}}(\tau,z)}

4.2
\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},t)

\sim
the case
z_{1}-z_{2}=(1+2p)\tau

Lemma 4.2
.

For
m\in{\mathbf{N}}
and
p\in{\mathbf{Z}}
, the following formula holds:

\displaystyle\hskip-25.60747pt\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\,\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}\Big(\tau,\,\ \frac{z}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}+p\tau,\,\ \frac{z}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}+p\tau,\,\ 0\Big)

(4.5)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{p}\,q^{\frac{m}{4}(2p+1)^{2}}\,\frac{\eta(\tau)^{3}}{\theta_{0,\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,z)}\cdot\big[\theta_{p,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}+\theta_{-p,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}\big](\tau,0)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ \bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,<\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,\leq\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{j-\frac{1}{2}}\,q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j+\frac{2pm}{2m+1})^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt\times\,\ q^{-\frac{1}{4m}\,[2mr+k+2mp]^{2}\,+\,\frac{m}{4}(2p+1)^{2}}\,\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ \bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,<\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{j-\frac{1}{2}}\,q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j+\frac{2pm}{2m+1})^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt\times\,\ q^{-\frac{1}{4m}\,[2mr-k+2mp]^{2}\,+\,\frac{m}{4}(2p+1)^{2}}\,\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt+\,\ \theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,m\end{subarray}}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}k^{2}+\frac{k}{2}(2p+1)}\,\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

Proof.

Letting
\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcl}z_{1}&=&\frac{z}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}+p\tau\\[0.0pt]
z_{2}&=&\frac{z}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}-p\tau\end{array}\right.
namely
\left\{\begin{array}[]{ccl}z_{1}+z_{2}&=&z\\[2.84526pt]
z_{1}-z_{2}&=&(2p+1)\tau\end{array}\right.
in the formula (
3.9
) in
Proposition
3.2
, we have

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt\theta_{\frac{1}{2},\,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}\Big(\tau,\,\ \frac{m(2p+1)\tau}{m+\frac{1}{2}}\Big)\,\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)

(4.6)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle i\,\eta(\tau)^{3}\Bigg\{\underbrace{-\,\frac{\displaystyle\theta_{-\frac{1}{2},\,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}\Big(\tau,\,\ z+\frac{(2p+1)\tau}{2m+1}\Big)}{\vartheta_{11}(\tau,z_{1})}+\frac{\displaystyle\theta_{\frac{1}{2},\,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}\Big(\tau,\,\ z-\frac{(2p+1)\tau}{2m+1}\Big)}{\vartheta_{11}(\tau,z_{2})}}_{\rm(I)}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ \bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,r\,\leq\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,<\,r\,\leq\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}s\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,s\,\leq\,m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{j}\,q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j+\frac{1}{4(m+\frac{1}{2})})^{2}}\,q^{-\frac{(2mr-s)^{2}}{4m}}

\displaystyle\times\,\ e^{2\pi im(j+\frac{1}{4(m+\frac{1}{2})})(2p+1)\tau}\,e^{-\pi i(2mr-s)(2p+1)\tau}\,\big[\theta_{s,m}+\theta_{-s,m}\big](\tau,z)\hskip 56.9055pt

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ \bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,<\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,\leq\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}s\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,s\,<\,m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{j}\,q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j+\frac{1}{4(m+\frac{1}{2})})^{2}}\,q^{-\frac{(2mr+s)^{2}}{4m}}

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ \bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]

j\,<\,r\,\leq\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}s\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,s\,\leq\,m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{j}\,q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j+\frac{1}{4(m+\frac{1}{2})})^{2}\,+\,m(j+\frac{1}{4(m+\frac{1}{2})})(2p+1)}

\displaystyle\times\,\ q^{-\frac{(2mr-s)^{2}}{4m}-\frac{1}{2}(2mr-s)(2p+1)}\,\big[\theta_{s,m}+\theta_{-s,m}\big](\tau,z)\hskip 56.9055pt

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ \bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,<\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,\leq\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}s\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,s\,<\,m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{j}\,q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j+\frac{1}{4(m+\frac{1}{2})})^{2}\,+\,m(j+\frac{1}{4(m+\frac{1}{2})})(2p+1)}

\displaystyle\times\,\ q^{-\frac{(2mr+s)^{2}}{4m}-\frac{1}{2}(2mr+s)(2p+1)}\,\big[\theta_{s,m}+\theta_{-s,m}\big](\tau,z)

The LHS of this equation (

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ \bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime},\,r^{\prime}\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r^{\prime}\,\leq\,j^{\prime}\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime},\,r^{\prime}\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
j^{\prime}\,<\,r^{\prime}\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{j^{\prime}-\frac{1}{2}}\,q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j^{\prime}+\frac{2pm}{2m+1})^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt\times\,\ q^{-\frac{1}{4m}\,[2mr^{\prime}-k+2mp]^{2}\,+\,\frac{m}{4}(2p+1)^{2}}\,\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt+\,\ \underbrace{\sum_{j\in{\mathbf{Z}}}(-1)^{j}\,q^{(m+\tfrac{1}{2})(j+\frac{m+\frac{1}{2}+2pm}{2m+1})^{2}}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[-5.69054pt]
{\displaystyle\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,m\end{subarray}}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}k^{2}+\frac{k}{2}(2p+1)}\,\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

proving Lemma
4.2
.
∎

5
\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}_{\rm add}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},t)\,\sim\,

the case
z_{1}-z_{2}=2a\tau+2b

Lemma 5.1
.

Let
m\in\frac{1}{2}{\mathbf{N}}
,
j,a\in\frac{1}{2}{\mathbf{Z}}
and
b\in{\mathbf{Q}}

such that
4mb\in{\mathbf{Z}}
. Then the functions

P_{j,m}(\tau,z):=P^{(+)}_{j,m}(\tau,z)

and
Q_{j,m}(\tau,z):=Q^{(+)}_{j,m}(\tau,z)
defined by the formulas
(4.1a) and (4.1b) in
[
17
]
satisfy the following:

1)

P_{j,m}(\tau,a\tau+b)\,+\,e^{4\pi ijb}\,e^{8\pi imab}\,P_{-j,m}(\tau,a\tau+b)\,\ =\,\ 0

2)

Q_{j,m}(\tau,a\tau+b)\,\ +\,\ e^{4\pi ijb}\,e^{8\pi imab}Q_{-j,m}(\tau,a\tau+b)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{2\pi ijb}\,e^{8\pi imab}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,2a\end{subarray}}e^{4\pi imbk}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}(j+2m(2a-k))(j-2mk)}

\displaystyle+\,\ e^{2\pi ijb}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,2a\end{subarray}}e^{4\pi imbk}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}(j-2m(2a-k))(j+2mk)}

Proof.

The claim 1) follows from (4.4) in
[
17
]
and
the claim 2) follows from (4.6b) in
[
17
]
,
since
e^{-8\pi imab}=e^{8\pi imab}
.
∎

Lemma 5.2
.

Let
m\in{\mathbf{N}}
,
a,b\in\frac{1}{2}{\mathbf{Z}}
and
j\in{\mathbf{Z}}
. Then

1)

R_{j,m}(\tau,\,a\tau+b)+R_{2m-j,m}(\tau,\,a\tau+b)\,\ =\,\ 2\,e^{2\pi ijb}\,\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
1\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,2a\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581ptq^{-\frac{1}{4m}(j+2m(2a-k))(j-2mk)}

2)

R_{0,m}(\tau,\,a\tau+b)\,\ =\,\ \sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,2a\end{subarray}}q^{mk(2a-k)}

Proof.

The claim 1) is obtained immediately
from Lemma 4.1 in
[
17
]
.

To prove the claim 2) we note, by letting
j=0
in Lemma

5.1
, that

P_{0,m}(\tau,\,a\tau+b)\,\ =\,\ 0

and that

\displaystyle Q_{0,m}(\tau,\,a\tau+b)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,2a\end{subarray}}\Big\{\underbrace{q^{-\frac{1}{4m}\cdot 2m(2a-k)(-2mk)}\,+\,q^{\frac{1}{4m}\cdot 2m(2a-k)\,2mk}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[0.0pt]
{\displaystyle 2\,q^{mk(2a-k)}}\end{subarray}}\Big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,2a\end{subarray}}q^{mk(2a-k)}

Thus we have

R_{0,m}(\tau,\,a\tau+b)\,=\,P_{0,m}(\tau,\,a\tau+b)+Q_{0,m}(\tau,\,a\tau+b)\,\ =\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,2a\end{subarray}}q^{mk(2a-k)}

proving Lemma
5.2
.
∎

Using the above Lemmas
5.1
and

5.2
, the Zwegers’ additional function

\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}_{\rm add}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)\,:=\,\Phi^{[m,0]}_{1,\,{\rm add}}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)+\Phi^{[m,0]}_{2,\,{\rm add}}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)

is obtained as follows:

Proposition 5.1
.

Let
m\in{\mathbf{N}}
and
a,b\in\frac{1}{2}{\mathbf{Z}}
.
Then, for
z_{1}
and
z_{2}
satisfying
z_{1}-z_{2}=2a\tau+2b
,
the correction function

\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}_{\rm add}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)

is given by the following formula:

\displaystyle\hskip-42.67912pt\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}_{\rm add}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)\,\ =\,\ -\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,2a\end{subarray}}q^{mk(2a-k)}\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})

\displaystyle-\,\ \frac{1}{2}\hskip-8.53581pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,j\,<\,2m\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581pte^{2\pi ijb}\hskip-5.69054pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
1\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,2a\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581ptq^{-\frac{1}{4m}(j+2m(2a-k))(j-2mk)}\big[\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}\big](\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})

(5.1)

Proof.

By the formula for
\Phi^{[m,0]}_{i,\,{\rm add}}
in

[
17
]
, we have

\displaystyle\hskip-22.76219pt\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}_{\rm add}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)\,=\,-\,\dfrac{1}{2}\hskip-14.22636pt\underbrace{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,j\,<\,2m\end{subarray}}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[-1.42262pt]
{\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}\,\ +\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,j\,<\,2m\end{subarray}}}\end{subarray}}\hskip-19.91692ptR_{j,m}(\tau,a\tau+b)\,\big[\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}\big](\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{2}\hskip-71.13188pt\underbrace{R_{0,m}(\tau,a\tau+b)}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[2.84526pt]
{\displaystyle\hskip 76.82243pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,2a\end{subarray}}q^{mk(2a-k)}\hskip 10.00002pt\text{by Lemma \ref{m1:lemma:2022-921a}}}\end{subarray}}\hskip-71.13188pt\times\,\ 2\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})

\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{4}\hskip-8.53581pt\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,j\,<\,2m\end{subarray}}R_{j,m}(\tau,a\tau+b)\,\big[\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}\big](\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})

\displaystyle-\,\ \frac{1}{4}\hskip-8.53581pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,j\,<\,2m\end{subarray}}R_{2m-j,m}(\tau,a\tau+b)\,\big[\underbrace{\theta_{2m-j,m}+\theta_{-(2m-j),m}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[0.0pt]
{\displaystyle\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}}\end{subarray}}\big](\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,2a\end{subarray}}q^{mk(2a-k)}\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})

\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{4}\hskip-8.53581pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,j\,<\,2m\end{subarray}}\big\{R_{2m-j,m}(\tau,a\tau+b)+R_{j,m}(\tau,a\tau+b)\big\}\,\big[\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}\big](\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})

Then, using Lemma
5.2
, this is rewritten as follows:

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,2a\end{subarray}}q^{mk(2a-k)}\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})

\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{4}\hskip-8.53581pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,j\,<\,2m\end{subarray}}2\,e^{2\pi ijb}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
1\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,2a\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581ptq^{-\frac{1}{4m}(j+2m(2a-k))(j-2mk)}\big[\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}\big](\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,2a\end{subarray}}q^{mk(2a-k)}\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})

\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{2}\hskip-8.53581pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,j\,<\,2m\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581pte^{2\pi ijb}\hskip-5.69054pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
1\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,2a\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581ptq^{-\frac{1}{4m}(j+2m(2a-k))(j-2mk)}\big[\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}\big](\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})

proving Proposition
5.1
.
∎

Using the above Proposition
5.1
, the Zwegers’s
additional functions
\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}_{\rm add}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)

for
(z_{1},z_{2})=\frac{z}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}-\frac{1}{2},\,\frac{z}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}+\frac{1}{2})
and

(z_{1},z_{2})=(\frac{z}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2},\,\frac{z}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2})
are obtained as follows:

Lemma 5.3
.

For
m\in{\mathbf{N}}
, the following formulas hold:

1)

\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}_{\rm add}\Big(\tau,\,\dfrac{z}{2}+\dfrac{\tau}{2}-\dfrac{1}{2},\,\dfrac{z}{2}-\dfrac{\tau}{2}+\dfrac{1}{2},\,0\Big)\,=\,-\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,j\,\leq\,2m\end{subarray}}\hskip-5.69054pt(-1)^{j}\,q^{-\frac{1}{4m}j(j-2m)}\theta_{j,m}(\tau,z)

2)

\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}_{\rm add}\Big(\tau,\,\dfrac{z}{2}+\dfrac{\tau}{2},\,\dfrac{z}{2}-\dfrac{\tau}{2},\,0\Big)\,=\,-\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,j\,\leq\,2m\end{subarray}}\hskip-5.69054ptq^{-\frac{1}{4m}j(j-2m)}\theta_{j,m}(\tau,z)

Proof.

1)   In the case

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcl}z_{1}&=&\frac{z}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\\[0.0pt]
z_{2}&=&\frac{z}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\end{array}\right.
, letting
\left\{\begin{array}[]{ccr}2a&=&1\\[0.0pt]
2b&=&-1\end{array}\right.
in (
5.1
), we have

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}_{\rm add}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)=\,\ -\,\underbrace{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,1\end{subarray}}q^{mk(1-k)}}_{2}\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})

\displaystyle-\,\ \frac{1}{2}\hskip-8.53581pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,j\,<\,2m\end{subarray}}\,\ e^{-\pi ij}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
1\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,1\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581ptq^{-\frac{1}{4m}(j+2m(1-k))(j-2mk)}\big[\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,2\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})\,\ -\,\ \frac{1}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,j\,<\,2m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{j}\,q^{-\frac{1}{4m}j(j-2m)}\big[\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,2\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)-\frac{1}{2}\hskip-8.53581pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,j\,<\,2m\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581pt(-1)^{j}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}j(j-2m)}\theta_{j,m}(\tau,z)-\frac{1}{2}\hskip-11.38109pt\underbrace{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,j\,<\,2m\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581pt(-1)^{j}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}j(j-2m)}\theta_{-j,m}(\tau,z)}_{\begin{subarray}{c}\hskip 54.06023pt||\,\ \leftarrow\,\ 2m-j\,=\,j^{\prime}\\[2.84526pt]
{\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,j^{\prime}\,<\,2m\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581pt(-1)^{j^{\prime}}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}j^{\prime}(j^{\prime}-2m)}\theta_{j^{\prime},m}(\tau,z)}\end{subarray}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,2\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)\,\ -\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,j\,<\,2m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{j}\,q^{-\frac{1}{4m}j(j-2m)}\theta_{j,m}(\tau,z)

proving 1).

2)   In the case
\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcl}z_{1}&=&\frac{z}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}\\[0.0pt]
z_{2}&=&\frac{z}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}\end{array}\right.
, letting
\left\{\begin{array}[]{ccr}2a&=&1\\[0.0pt]
2b&=&0\end{array}\right.
in (
5.1
), we have

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}_{\rm add}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)=\,-\,\underbrace{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,1\end{subarray}}q^{mk(1-k)}}_{2}\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})

\displaystyle-\,\ \frac{1}{2}\hskip-8.53581pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,j\,<\,2m\end{subarray}}\underbrace{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
1\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,1\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581ptq^{-\frac{1}{4m}(j+2m(1-k))(j-2mk)}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[0.0pt]
{\displaystyle q^{-\frac{1}{4m}j(j-2m)}}\end{subarray}}\big[\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,2\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z_{1}+z_{2})\,\ -\,\ \frac{1}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,j\,<\,2m\end{subarray}}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}j(j-2m)}\big[\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,2\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)-\frac{1}{2}\hskip-8.53581pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,j\,<\,2m\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581ptq^{-\frac{1}{4m}j(j-2m)}\theta_{j,m}(\tau,z)-\frac{1}{2}\hskip-8.53581pt\underbrace{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,j\,<\,2m\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581ptq^{-\frac{1}{4m}j(j-2m)}\theta_{-j,m}(\tau,z)}_{\begin{subarray}{c}\hskip 54.06023pt||\,\ \leftarrow\,\ 2m-j\,=\,j^{\prime}\\[2.84526pt]
{\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,j^{\prime}\,<\,2m\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581ptq^{-\frac{1}{4m}j^{\prime}(j^{\prime}-2m)}\theta_{j^{\prime},m}(\tau,z)}\end{subarray}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,2\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)\,\ -\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,j\,<\,2m\end{subarray}}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}j(j-2m)}\theta_{j,m}(\tau,z)

proving 2).
∎

6
\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1}+p\tau,z_{2}-p\tau,0)

6.1
\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1}+p\tau,z_{2}-p\tau,t)

\sim
the case
z_{1}-z_{2}=\tau-1

Lemma 6.1
.

For
m\in{\mathbf{N}}
and
p\in{\mathbf{Z}}
, the following formula holds:

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}\Big(\tau,\,\ \frac{z}{2}+\dfrac{\tau}{2}-\dfrac{1}{2}+p\tau,\,\ \frac{z}{2}-\dfrac{\tau}{2}+\dfrac{1}{2}-p\tau,\,\ 0\Big)

(6.1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle q^{mp(p+1)}\,\bigg\{\widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}\Big(\tau,\,\ \frac{z}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}-\frac{1}{2},\,\ \frac{z}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}+\frac{1}{2},\,\ 0\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{k}\,q^{-\frac{1}{4m}(2mr+k)(2m(r-1)+k)}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ 2\hskip-5.69054pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p-1\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581ptq^{-mr(r+1)}\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)\,+\,(-1)^{m}\hskip-8.53581pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581ptq^{-\frac{m}{4}(2r+1)(2r-1)}\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)\bigg\}

Proof.

Letting
\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcc}z_{1}&=&\frac{z}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\\[2.84526pt]
z_{2}&=&\frac{z}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\end{array}\right.
namely

\left\{\begin{array}[]{ccc}z_{1}-z_{2}&=&\tau-1\\[0.0pt]
z_{1}+z_{2}&=&z\end{array}\right.
in the formula (
2.2
)
in Lemma
2.2
, we have

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,\,z_{1}+p\tau,\,z_{2}-p\tau,\,0)

(6.2)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{2\pi imp(\tau-1)}q^{mp^{2}}\bigg\{\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
1\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,2pm\end{subarray}}e^{-\pi ik(\tau-1)}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}k^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle q^{mp(p+1)}\bigg\{\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)+\hskip-5.69054pt\underbrace{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
1\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,2pm\end{subarray}}\hskip-11.38109pt(-1)^{k}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}(k^{2}+2mk)}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)}_{\rm(I)}\bigg\}

We compute (I) by putting
k=2mr+k^{\prime}

(0\leq r<p,\,\ 1\leq k^{\prime}\leq 2m)
as follows:

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt{\rm(I)}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\leq r<p\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k^{\prime}\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
1\,\leq\,k^{\prime}\,\leq\,2m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{2mr+k^{\prime}}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}(2mr+k^{\prime})(2mr+k^{\prime}+2m)}\big[\theta_{2mr+k^{\prime},m}+\theta_{-(2mr+k^{\prime}),m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,<\,p\end{subarray}}\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k^{\prime}\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
1\,\leq\,k^{\prime}\,\leq\,2m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{k^{\prime}}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}(2mr+k^{\prime})(2m(r+1)+k^{\prime})}\,\theta_{k^{\prime},m}(\tau,z)}_{(A)}

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt+\,\ \underbrace{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,<\,p\end{subarray}}\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k^{\prime}\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
1\,\leq\,k^{\prime}\,\leq\,2m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{k^{\prime}}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}(2mr+k^{\prime})(2m(r+1)+k^{\prime})}\,\theta_{-k^{\prime},m}(\tau,z)}_{(B)}

where
(A)
is computed by using

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
1\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,2m\end{subarray}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,<\,2m\end{subarray}}-\,\ \sum_{k\,=\,0}\,\ +\,\ \sum_{k\,=\,2m}

(6.3)

as follows:

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91692pt(A)\,\ =\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,<\,p\end{subarray}}\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]

),
we have

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,\,z_{1}+p\tau,\,z_{2}-p\tau,\,0)\,\ =\,\ q^{mp(p+1)}\,\Big\{\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)\,\ +\,\ {\rm(I)}\Big\}

(6.11)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle q^{mp(p+1)}\,\bigg\{\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)

\displaystyle+\,\ \sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,<\,2m\end{subarray}}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}(2mr+k)(2m(r-1)+k)}\,\theta_{k,m}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-34.1433pt\underbrace{-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,2m\end{subarray}}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}k(k-2m)}\theta_{k,m}(\tau,z)}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[-1.42262pt]
{\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}_{\rm add}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)\hskip 10.00002pt\text{by Lemma \ref{m1:lemma:2022-921c}}}\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581pt+\quad q^{-mp(p+1)}\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle q^{mp(p+1)}\,\bigg\{\widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)+\hskip-8.53581pt\underbrace{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,<\,2m\end{subarray}}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}(2mr+k)(2m(r-1)+k)}\theta_{k,m}(\tau,z)}_{\rm(II)}

\displaystyle+\,\ q^{-mp(p+1)}\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)\bigg\}

(6.12)

We go further to compute

{\rm(II)}\,\ =\,\ {\rm(II)}_{A}+{\rm(II)}_{B}

where

\displaystyle{\rm(II)}_{A}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\,\ \sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,m\end{subarray}}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}(2mr+k)(2m(r-1)+k)}\theta_{k,m}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle{\rm(II)}_{B}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\,\ \sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
m\,<\,k\,<\,2m\end{subarray}}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}(2mr+k)(2m(r-1)+k)}\theta_{k,m}(\tau,z)

First we compute
{\rm(II)}_{A}
:

\displaystyle{\rm(II)}_{A}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\,\underbrace{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,m\end{subarray}}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[-1.42262pt]
{\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}+\sum_{k=m}}\end{subarray}}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}(2mr+k)(2m(r-1)+k)}\theta_{k,m}(\tau,z)

(6.13a)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}(2mr+k)(2m(r-1)+k)}\theta_{k,m}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle+\,\ \sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}q^{-\frac{m}{4}(2r+1)(2r-1)}\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)

Next,
{\rm(II)}_{B}
is rewritten as follows by putting
k=2m-k^{\prime}
:

\displaystyle{\rm(II)}_{B}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k^{\prime}\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,k^{\prime}\,<\,m\end{subarray}}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}(2m(r+1)-k^{\prime})(2mr-k^{\prime})}\theta_{-k^{\prime},m}(\tau,z)

(6.13b)

\displaystyle\hskip-5.69054pt\underset{\begin{subarray}{c}\\[1.42262pt]
\uparrow\\[2.84526pt]
r=-r^{\prime}\end{subarray}}{=}\hskip-5.69054pt

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r^{\prime}\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r^{\prime}\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\underbrace{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k^{\prime}\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,k^{\prime}\,<\,m\end{subarray}}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[-1.42262pt]
{\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k^{\prime}\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k^{\prime}\,<\,m\end{subarray}}-\sum_{k^{\prime}=0}}\end{subarray}}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}(2m(r^{\prime}-1)+k^{\prime})(2mr^{\prime}+k^{\prime})}\theta_{-k^{\prime},m}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r^{\prime}\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r^{\prime}\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k^{\prime}\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k^{\prime}\,<\,m\end{subarray}}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}(2m(r^{\prime}-1)+k^{\prime})(2mr^{\prime}+k^{\prime})}\theta_{-k^{\prime},m}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-99.58464pt\underbrace{-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r^{\prime}\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r^{\prime}\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}q^{-mr^{\prime}(r^{\prime}-1)}\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[0.0pt]
{\displaystyle\hskip 108.12047pt-\,2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p-1\end{subarray}}\hskip-5.69054ptq^{-mr(r+1)}\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)\,-\,q^{-mp(p+1)}\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)}\end{subarray}}

Then by (
6.13a
) and (
6.13b
),
we have

\displaystyle\hskip-34.1433pt{\rm(II)}+q^{-mp(p+1)}\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)

(6.14)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}(2mr+k)(2m(r-1)+k)}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle-\,2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p-1\end{subarray}}q^{-mr(r+1)}\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)\,+\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}q^{-\frac{m}{4}(2r+1)(2r-1)}\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)

Substituting this equation (
6.14
) into
(
6.9
), we obtain

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt\Phi^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,\,z_{1}+p\tau,\,z_{2}-p\tau,\,0)\,\ =\,\ q^{mp(p+1)}\,\bigg\{\widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}(\tau,z_{1},z_{2},0)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{k}\,q^{-\frac{1}{4m}(2mr+k)(2m(r-1)+k)}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle-\,\ 2\hskip-5.69054pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p-1\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581ptq^{-mr(r+1)}\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)\,+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581ptq^{-\frac{m}{4}(2r+1)(2r-1)}\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)\bigg\}

proving Lemma
6.2
.
∎

7
Modified function
\widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}

with specialization

Proposition 7.1
.

For
m\in{\mathbf{N}}
and
p\in{\mathbf{Z}}_{\geq 0}
, the following formulas hold:

1)

(-1)^{p}\,q^{-\frac{m}{4}}\,\theta_{p-m-\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\,\ \widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}\Big(\tau,\,\ \dfrac{z}{2}+\dfrac{\tau}{2}-\dfrac{1}{2},\,\ \dfrac{z}{2}-\dfrac{\tau}{2}+\dfrac{1}{2},\,\ 0\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\eta(\tau)^{3}}{\theta_{0,\frac{1}{2}}(\tau,z)}\cdot\big[\theta_{p,m+\frac{1}{2}}+\theta_{-p,m+\frac{1}{2}}\big](\tau,0)

(7.1a)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ \bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,<\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,\leq\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{j-\frac{1}{2}+k}\,q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j+\frac{2pm}{2m+1})^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\times\,\ q^{-\frac{1}{4m}\,[2mr+k+2mp]^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ \bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,<\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{j-\frac{1}{2}+k}\,q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j+\frac{2pm}{2m+1})^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\times\,\ q^{-\frac{1}{4m}\,[2mr-k+2mp]^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-17.07164pt-\,(-1)^{p}\,\theta_{p-m-\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\hskip-8.53581pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,<\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\,\ \sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}\hskip-5.69054pt(-1)^{k}q^{-\,\frac{1}{4m}\,(m(2r-1)+k)^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt+\,\ 2\,(-1)^{p}\,\theta_{p-m-\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p-1\end{subarray}}q^{-m(r+\frac{1}{2})^{2}}\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ (-1)^{m+p}\,\theta_{p-m-\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,<\,r\,<\,p\end{subarray}}q^{-mr^{2}}\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)

2)

(-1)^{p}\,q^{-\frac{m}{4}}\,\theta_{-p+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\,\ \widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}\Big(\tau,\,\ \dfrac{z}{2}+\dfrac{\tau}{2},\,\ \dfrac{z}{2}-\dfrac{\tau}{2},\,\ 0\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{p}\,\frac{\eta(\tau)^{3}}{\theta_{0,\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,z)}\cdot\big[\theta_{p,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}+\theta_{-p,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}\big](\tau,0)

(7.1b)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ \bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,<\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,\leq\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{j-\frac{1}{2}}\,q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j+\frac{2pm}{2m+1})^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\times\,\ q^{-\frac{1}{4m}\,[2mr+k+2mp]^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ \bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leqq\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,<\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{j-\frac{1}{2}}\,q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j+\frac{2pm}{2m+1})^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\times\,\ q^{-\frac{1}{4m}\,[2mr-k+2mp]^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ \theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\hskip-8.53581pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,<\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\,\ \sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}\hskip-5.69054ptq^{-\,\frac{1}{4m}\,(m(2r-1)+k)^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt+\,\ 2\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p-1\end{subarray}}q^{-m(r+\frac{1}{2})^{2}}\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ \theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,<\,r\,<\,p\end{subarray}}q^{-mr^{2}}\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)

Proof.

1) In the case
\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcc}z_{1}&=&\frac{z}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\\[2.84526pt]
z_{2}&=&\frac{z}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\end{array}\right.
, substituting (
6.1
) into
(
4.1
), we have

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91692pt\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\,\ \times\,\ \Bigg\{q^{mp(p+1)}\,\widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}\Big(\tau,\,\ \frac{z}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}-\frac{1}{2},\,\ \frac{z}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}+\frac{1}{2},\,\ 0\Big)

\displaystyle+\,\ q^{mp(p+1)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}\hskip-5.69054pt(-1)^{k}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}(2mr+k)(2m(r-1)+k)}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle-\,\ 2\,q^{mp(p+1)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p-1\end{subarray}}q^{-mr(r+1)}\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle+\,\ (-1)^{m}\,q^{mp(p+1)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}q^{-\frac{m}{4}(2r+1)(2r-1)}\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle q^{\frac{m}{4}(2p+1)^{2}}\,\frac{\eta(\tau)^{3}}{\theta_{0,\frac{1}{2}}(\tau,z)}\cdot\big[\theta_{p,m+\frac{1}{2}}+\theta_{-p,m+\frac{1}{2}}\big](\tau,0)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ q^{\frac{m}{4}(2p+1)^{2}}\,\bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,<\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,\leq\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{j-\frac{1}{2}+k}\,q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j+\frac{2pm}{2m+1})^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\times\,\ q^{-\frac{1}{4m}\,[2mr+k+2mp]^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ q^{\frac{m}{4}(2p+1)^{2}}\,\bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,<\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{j-\frac{1}{2}+k}\,q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j+\frac{2pm}{2m+1})^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\times\,\ q^{-\frac{1}{4m}\,[2mr-k+2mp]^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt+\,\ \theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{k}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}k^{2}+\frac{k}{2}(2p+1)}\,\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

Multiplying
q^{-\frac{m}{4}(2p+1)^{2}}\,=\,q^{-mp(p+1)-\frac{m}{4}}

to both sides, we have

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636ptq^{-\frac{m}{4}}\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\,\widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}\Big(\tau,\,\ \frac{z}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}-\frac{1}{2},\,\ \frac{z}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}+\frac{1}{2},\,\ 0\Big)

\displaystyle+\,\ q^{-\frac{m}{4}}\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\,

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\times\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}\hskip-5.69054pt(-1)^{k}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}(2mr+k)(2m(r-1)+k)}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle-\,\ 2\,q^{-\frac{m}{4}}\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p-1\end{subarray}}q^{-mr(r+1)}\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle+\,\ (-1)^{m}\,q^{-\frac{m}{4}}\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}q^{-\frac{m}{4}(2r+1)(2r-1)}\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\eta(\tau)^{3}}{\theta_{0,\frac{1}{2}}(\tau,z)}\cdot\big[\theta_{p,m+\frac{1}{2}}+\theta_{-p,m+\frac{1}{2}}\big](\tau,0)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ \bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,<\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,\leq\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{j-\frac{1}{2}+k}\,q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j+\frac{2pm}{2m+1})^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\times\,\ q^{-\frac{1}{4m}\,[2mr+k+2mp]^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ \bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,<\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{j-\frac{1}{2}+k}\,q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j+\frac{2pm}{2m+1})^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\times\,\ q^{-\frac{1}{4m}\,[2mr-k+2mp]^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt+\,\ \theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{k}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}[k-m(2p+1)]^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

namely

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91692ptq^{-\frac{m}{4}}\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\,\widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}\Big(\tau,\,\ \frac{z}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}-\frac{1}{2},\,\ \frac{z}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}+\frac{1}{2},\,\ 0\Big)

(7.2a)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\eta(\tau)^{3}}{\theta_{0,\frac{1}{2}}(\tau,z)}\cdot\big[\theta_{p,m+\frac{1}{2}}+\theta_{-p,m+\frac{1}{2}}\big](\tau,0)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ \bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,<\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,\leq\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{j-\frac{1}{2}+k}\,q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j+\frac{2pm}{2m+1})^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\times\,\ q^{-\frac{1}{4m}\,[2mr+k+2mp]^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ \bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,<\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{j-\frac{1}{2}+k}\,q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j+\frac{2pm}{2m+1})^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\times\,\ q^{-\frac{1}{4m}\,[2mr-k+2mp]^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt+\,\ \underbrace{\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{k}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}[k-m(2p+1)]^{2}}\,\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)}_{\rm(I)_{A}}

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt\underbrace{-\,q^{-\frac{m}{4}}\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\hskip-5.69054pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}\hskip-5.69054pt(-1)^{k}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}(2mr+k)(2m(r-1)+k)}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)}_{\rm(I)_{B}}

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt+\,\ 2\,q^{-\frac{m}{4}}\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p-1\end{subarray}}q^{-mr(r+1)}\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ (-1)^{m}\,q^{-\frac{m}{4}}\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}q^{-mr^{2}+\frac{m}{4}}\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)

Since

-\,\frac{1}{4m}\,(2mr+k)(2m(r-1)+k)\,\ -\,\ \frac{m}{4}\,\ =\,\ -\,\frac{1}{4m}\big(m(2r-1)+k\big)^{2}

{\rm(I)}_{B}
becomes as follows:

\displaystyle\hskip-22.76219pt{\rm(I)}_{B}=-\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\hskip-14.22636pt\underbrace{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[-1.42262pt]
{\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,<\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}+\sum_{r=-p}}\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}\hskip-5.69054pt(-1)^{k}q^{-\,\frac{1}{4m}\,(m(2r-1)+k)^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,<\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}\hskip-5.69054pt(-1)^{k}q^{-\,\frac{1}{4m}\,\big(m(2r-1)+k\big)^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\underbrace{-\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}\hskip-5.69054pt(-1)^{k}q^{-\,\frac{1}{4m}\,\big(-m(2p+1)+k\big)^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)}_{\begin{subarray}{c}\uparrow\\[2.84526pt]
{\displaystyle-\,{\rm(I)}_{A}}\end{subarray}}

so

\displaystyle{\rm(I)}_{A}+{\rm(I)}_{B}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\hskip-8.53581pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,<\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}\hskip-5.69054pt(-1)^{k}q^{-\,\frac{1}{4m}\,(m(2r-1)+k)^{2}}

(7.2b)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\,\ \big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

Substituting (

) into (
7.2a
)
and noticing that

(7.3)

we obtain (

), proving the claim 1).

2) In the case
\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcc}z_{1}&=&\frac{z}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}\\[2.84526pt]

, substituting (

) into

), we have

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91692pt\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\,\ \times\,\ \Bigg\{q^{mp(p+1)}\,\widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}\Big(\tau,\,\ \frac{z}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2},\,\ \frac{z}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2},\,\ 0\Big)

\displaystyle+\,\ q^{mp(p+1)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]

\displaystyle-\,\ 2\,q^{mp(p+1)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]

\displaystyle+\,\ q^{mp(p+1)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}q^{-\frac{m}{4}(2r+1)(2r-1)}\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{p}\,q^{\frac{m}{4}(2p+1)^{2}}\,\frac{\eta(\tau)^{3}}{\theta_{0,\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,z)}\cdot\big[\theta_{p,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}+\theta_{-p,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}\big](\tau,0)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ q^{\frac{m}{4}(2p+1)^{2}}\,\bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\times\,\ q^{-\frac{1}{4m}\,[2mr+k+2mp]^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ q^{\frac{m}{4}(2p+1)^{2}}\,\bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\times\,\ q^{-\frac{1}{4m}\,[2mr-k+2mp]^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt+\,\ \theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]

to both sides, we have

\displaystyle q^{-\frac{m}{4}}\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\,\widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}\Big(\tau,\,\ \frac{z}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}-\frac{1}{2},\,\ \frac{z}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}+\frac{1}{2},\,\ 0\Big)

\displaystyle+\,\ q^{-\frac{m}{4}}\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\,

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\times\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]

\displaystyle-\,\ 2\,q^{-\frac{m}{4}}\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p-1\end{subarray}}q^{-mr(r+1)}\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle+\,\ q^{-\frac{m}{4}}\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{p}\,\ \frac{\eta(\tau)^{3}}{\theta_{0,\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,z)}\cdot\big[\theta_{p,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}+\theta_{-p,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}\big](\tau,0)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ \bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\times\,\ q^{-\frac{1}{4m}\,[2mr+k+2mp]^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ \bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]

namely

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91692ptq^{-\frac{m}{4}}\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\,\widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}\Big(\tau,\,\ \frac{z}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}-\frac{1}{2},\,\ \frac{z}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}+\frac{1}{2},\,\ 0\Big)

(7.4a)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{p}\,\ \frac{\eta(\tau)^{3}}{\theta_{0,\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,z)}\cdot\big[\theta_{p,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}+\theta_{-p,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}\big](\tau,0)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ \bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,<\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,\leq\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{j-\frac{1}{2}}\,q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j+\frac{2pm}{2m+1})^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\times\,\ q^{-\frac{1}{4m}\,[2mr+k+2mp]^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ \bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
