j\,<\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{j-\frac{1}{2}}\,q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j+\frac{2pm}{2m+1})^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\times\,\ q^{-\frac{1}{4m}\,[2mr-k+2mp]^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt+\,\ \underbrace{\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,m\end{subarray}}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}[k-m(2p+1)]^{2}}\,\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)}_{\rm(I)_{A}}

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt\underbrace{-\,q^{-\frac{m}{4}}\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\hskip-5.69054pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}\hskip-5.69054ptq^{-\frac{1}{4m}(2mr+k)(2m(r-1)+k)}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)}_{\rm(I)_{B}}

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt+\,\ 2\,q^{-\frac{m}{4}}\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p-1\end{subarray}}q^{-mr(r+1)}\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt-\,\ q^{-\frac{m}{4}}\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}q^{-mr^{2}+\frac{m}{4}}\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)

where
{\rm(I)}_{B}
becomes as follows:

\displaystyle\hskip-22.76219pt{\rm(I)}_{B}=-\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\hskip-14.22636pt\underbrace{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[-4.2679pt]
{\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,<\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}+\sum_{r=-p}}\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}\hskip-5.69054ptq^{-\,\frac{1}{4m}\,\big(m(2r-1)+k\big)^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,<\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\,\ \sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}\hskip-5.69054ptq^{-\,\frac{1}{4m}\,\big(m(2r-1)+k\big)^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\underbrace{-\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}\hskip-5.69054ptq^{-\,\frac{1}{4m}\,\big(-m(2p+1)+k\big)^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[0.0pt]
{\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt-\,{\rm(I)}_{A}}\end{subarray}}

so

\displaystyle{\rm(I)}_{A}+{\rm(I)}_{B}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\hskip-5.69054pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,<\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\,\ \sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}\hskip-2.84526ptq^{-\,\frac{1}{4m}\,\big(m(2r-1)+k\big)^{2}}

(7.4b)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\,\ \big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

Substituting (
7.4b
) into (
7.4a
)
and using (
7.3
), we obtain
(
7.1b
), proving the claim 2).
∎

8
Modular transformation of
\widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}

8.1
\widetilde{\psi}^{(i)[m]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

For
m\in{\mathbf{N}}
and
i\in\{1,2\}
, we consider functions

\widetilde{\psi}^{(i)[m]\,\ast}(\tau,z)
defined by

\displaystyle\widetilde{\psi}^{(1)[m]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}\Big(\tau,\,\ \frac{z}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}-\frac{1}{2},\,\ \frac{z}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}+\frac{1}{2},\,\ 0\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}\Big(\tau,\,\ \frac{z}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}+\frac{1}{2},\,\ \frac{z}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}-\frac{1}{2},\,\ 0\Big)

\displaystyle\widetilde{\psi}^{(2)[m]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}\Big(\tau,\,\ \frac{z}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2},\,\ \frac{z}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2},\,\ 0\Big)

The function
\widetilde{\psi}^{(1)[m]\,\ast}(\tau,z)
satisfies
the follwing modular transformation properties.

Lemma 8.1
.

Let
m\in{\mathbf{N}}
, then

1)

\widetilde{\psi}^{(1)[m]\,\ast}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau},\dfrac{z}{\tau}\Big)\,\ =\,\ (-1)^{m}\,\tau\,e^{\frac{\pi im}{2\tau}z^{2}}\,e^{-\frac{\pi im}{2\tau}}\,q^{-\frac{m}{4}}\,\widetilde{\psi}^{(1)[m]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

2)

\widetilde{\psi}^{(1)[m]\,\ast}(\tau+1,z)\,\ =\,\ \widetilde{\psi}^{(2)[m]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

Proof.

These are obtained easily from Lemma 2.1 in
[
17
]

as follows.

1)
\widetilde{\psi}^{(1)[m]\,\ast}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau},\dfrac{z}{\tau}\Big)=\widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau},\dfrac{z}{2\tau}-\dfrac{1}{2\tau}+\dfrac{1}{2},\dfrac{z}{2\tau}+\dfrac{1}{2\tau}-\dfrac{1}{2},0\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau},\,\frac{\frac{z}{2}-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}{\tau},\,\frac{\frac{z}{2}+\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}{\tau},\,0\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tau\,e^{\frac{2\pi im}{\tau}(\frac{z}{2}-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2})(\frac{z}{2}+\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2})}\widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}\Big(\tau,\frac{z}{2}-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2},\frac{z}{2}+\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2},0\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{m}\,\tau\,e^{\frac{\pi im}{2\tau}z^{2}}e^{-\frac{\pi im}{2\tau}}q^{-\frac{m}{4}}\widetilde{\psi}^{(1)[m]\,\ast}(\tau,z)\,,\hskip 28.45274pt\text{proving 1)}.

2)
\widetilde{\psi}^{(1)[m]\,\ast}(\tau+1,z)\,=\,\widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}\Big(\tau+1,\,\dfrac{z}{2}+\dfrac{\tau+1}{2}-\dfrac{1}{2},\,\dfrac{z}{2}-\dfrac{\tau+1}{2}+\dfrac{1}{2},\,0\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}\Big(\tau+1,\,\frac{z}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2},\,\frac{z}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2},\,0\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\widetilde{\Phi}^{[m,0]\,\ast}\Big(\tau,\,\frac{z}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2},\,\frac{z}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2},\,0\Big)\,=\,\widetilde{\psi}^{(2)[m]\,\ast}(\tau,z)\,,\hskip 28.45274pt\text{proving 2)}.

∎

8.2
\Xi^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)
and

\Upsilon^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

For
m\in{\mathbf{N}}
and
p\in{\mathbf{Z}}
such that

0\leq p\leq 2m
and
i\in\{1,2,3\}
, we define functions

\Xi^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

and
\Upsilon^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)
as follows:

\displaystyle\Xi^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle(-1)^{p}\,q^{-\frac{m}{4}}\,\theta_{p-m-\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\cdot\widetilde{\psi}^{(1)[m]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

(8.1a)

\displaystyle\Xi^{(2)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle q^{-\frac{m}{4}}\,\theta_{p-m-\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\cdot\widetilde{\psi}^{(2)[m]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

(8.1b)

and

\displaystyle\Upsilon^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\eta(\tau)^{3}\,\cdot\frac{\theta_{p,m+\frac{1}{2}}(\tau,z)\,+\,\theta_{-p,m+\frac{1}{2}}(\tau,z)}{\theta_{0,\frac{1}{2}}(\tau,z)}

(8.2a)

\displaystyle\Upsilon^{(2)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\eta(\tau)^{3}\,\cdot\frac{\theta_{p,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,z)\,+\,\theta_{-p,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,z)}{\theta_{0,\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,z)}

(8.2b)

To compute modular transformation of these functions,
we use the following formulas which are obtained easily from
Lemmas 1.3 and 1.4 in
[
17
]
.

Note 8.1
.

Let
m\in{\mathbf{Z}}_{\geq 0}
and
p\in{\mathbf{Z}}
. Then

1)

(i)

\theta_{p-m-\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau},0\Big)=\,-\,i\,(-1)^{m+p}\,\dfrac{(-i\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\sqrt{2m+1}}\sum\limits_{p^{\prime}=0}^{2m}(-1)^{p^{\prime}}e^{-\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}pp^{\prime}}\theta_{p^{\prime}-m-\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)

(ii)

\theta_{p-m-\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau+1,\,z)\,\ =\,\ (-1)^{p}\,e^{\frac{\pi i}{2}(m+\frac{1}{2})}\,e^{\frac{\pi i}{2m+1}p^{2}}\,\theta_{p-m-\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,z)

2)

(i)

\big[\theta_{p,m+\frac{1}{2}}+\theta_{-p,m+\frac{1}{2}}\big]\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau},\dfrac{z}{\tau}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(-i\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\sqrt{2m+1}}\,e^{\frac{\pi i}{2\tau}(m+\frac{1}{2})z^{2}}\sum_{p^{\prime}\in{\mathbf{Z}}/(2m+1){\mathbf{Z}}}e^{\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}pp^{\prime}}\,\big[\theta_{p^{\prime},m+\frac{1}{2}}+\theta_{-p^{\prime},m+\frac{1}{2}}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(-i\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\sqrt{2m+1}}\,e^{\frac{\pi i}{2\tau}(m+\frac{1}{2})z^{2}}\sum_{p^{\prime}\in{\mathbf{Z}}/(2m+1){\mathbf{Z}}}e^{-\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}pp^{\prime}}\,\big[\theta_{p^{\prime},m+\frac{1}{2}}+\theta_{-p^{\prime},m+\frac{1}{2}}\big](\tau,z)

(ii)

\big[\theta_{p,m+\frac{1}{2}}+\theta_{-p,m+\frac{1}{2}}\big](\tau+1,z)\,=\,e^{\frac{\pi i}{2m+1}p^{2}}\,\big[\theta_{p,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}+\theta_{-p,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}\big](\tau,z)

Then modular transformation properties of
\Xi^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}

and
\Upsilon^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}
are given by the following formulas:

Lemma 8.2
.

Let
m\in{\mathbf{N}}
and
p\in{\mathbf{Z}}
. Then

1)

\Xi^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau},\dfrac{z}{\tau}\Big)\,=\,\dfrac{(-i\tau)^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\sqrt{2m+1}}\,e^{\frac{\pi im}{2\tau}z^{2}}\,\sum\limits_{p^{\prime}=0}^{2m}e^{-\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}pp^{\prime}}\,\Xi^{(1)[m,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

2)

\Xi^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau+1,z)\,=\,e^{\frac{\pi i}{4}}\,e^{\frac{\pi i}{2m+1}p^{2}}\,\Xi^{(2)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

Proof.

1)
\Xi^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau},\dfrac{z}{\tau}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{p}e^{-2\pi i\frac{m}{4}(-\frac{1}{\tau})}\,\theta_{p-m-\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau},0\Big)\,\widetilde{\psi}^{(1)[m]\,\ast}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau},\dfrac{z}{\tau}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{p}\,e^{\frac{\pi im}{2\tau}}\,\times\,\bigg\{-\,i\,(-1)^{m+p}\,\frac{(-i\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\sqrt{2m+1}}\sum_{p^{\prime}=0}^{2m}(-1)^{p^{\prime}}e^{-\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}pp^{\prime}}\theta_{p^{\prime}-m-\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\bigg\}

\displaystyle\times\,\ (-1)^{m}\,\tau\,e^{\frac{\pi im}{2\tau}z^{2}}\,e^{-\frac{\pi im}{2\tau}}\,q^{-\frac{m}{4}}\,\widetilde{\psi}^{(1)[m]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(-i\tau)^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\sqrt{2m+1}}\,e^{\frac{\pi im}{2\tau}z^{2}}\,\sum_{p^{\prime}=0}^{2m}e^{-\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}pp^{\prime}}\,\underbrace{(-1)^{p^{\prime}}\,q^{-\frac{m}{4}}\,\theta_{p^{\prime}-m-\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\,\widetilde{\psi}^{(1)[m]\,\ast}(\tau,z)}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[0.0pt]
{\displaystyle\Xi^{(1)[m,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}(\tau,z)}\end{subarray}}

proving 1).

2)
\Xi^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau+1,z)\,=\,(-1)^{p}\,e^{-\frac{\pi im}{2}(\tau+1)}\,\theta_{p-m-\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau+1,0)\,\widetilde{\psi}^{(1)[m]\,\ast}(\tau+1,z)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{p}\,q^{-\frac{m}{4}}\,e^{-\frac{\pi im}{2}}\,\times\,\Big\{(-1)^{p}\,e^{\frac{\pi i}{2}(m+\frac{1}{2})}\,e^{\frac{\pi i}{2m+1}p^{2}}\,\theta_{p-m-\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,z)\Big\}\,\widetilde{\psi}^{(2)[m]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\frac{\pi i}{4}}\,e^{\frac{\pi i}{2m+1}p^{2}}\,\underbrace{q^{-\frac{m}{4}}\,\theta_{p-m-\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,z)\,\widetilde{\psi}^{(2)[m]\,\ast}(\tau,z)}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[0.0pt]
{\displaystyle\Xi^{(2)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)}\end{subarray}}\,,\hskip 28.45274pt\text{proving 2).}

∎

Lemma 8.3
.

Let
m\in{\mathbf{N}}
and
p\in{\mathbf{Z}}
. Then

1)

\Upsilon^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau},\dfrac{z}{\tau}\Big)\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{(-i\tau)^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\sqrt{2m+1}}\,e^{\frac{\pi im}{2\tau}z^{2}}\sum\limits_{p^{\prime}=0}^{2m}e^{-\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}pp^{\prime}}\,\Upsilon^{(1)[m,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

2)

\Upsilon^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau+1,z)\,\ =\,\ e^{\frac{\pi i}{2m+1}p^{2}+\frac{\pi i}{4}}\,\Upsilon^{(2)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

Proof.

1)
\Upsilon^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau},\dfrac{z}{\tau}\Big)=\eta(-\frac{1}{\tau})^{3}\cdot\dfrac{\theta_{p,m+\frac{1}{2}}(-\frac{1}{\tau},\frac{z}{\tau})\,+\,\theta_{-p,m+\frac{1}{2}}(-\frac{1}{\tau},\frac{z}{\tau})}{\theta_{0,\frac{1}{2}}(-\frac{1}{\tau},\,\frac{z}{\tau})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-i\tau)^{\frac{3}{2}}\eta(\tau)^{3}\,\frac{(-i\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\sqrt{2m+1}}\,e^{\frac{\pi i}{2\tau}(m+\frac{1}{2})z^{2}}\hskip-14.22636pt\sum_{p^{\prime}\in{\mathbf{Z}}(2m+1){\mathbf{Z}}}\hskip-14.22636pte^{-\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}pp^{\prime}}\,\frac{\big[\theta_{p^{\prime},m+\frac{1}{2}}+\theta_{-p^{\prime},m+\frac{1}{2}}\big](\tau,z)}{(-i\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}}\,e^{\frac{\pi iz^{2}}{4\tau}}\,\theta_{0,\frac{1}{2}}(\tau,z)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(-i\tau)^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\sqrt{2m+1}}\,e^{\frac{\pi im}{2\tau}z^{2}}\sum_{p^{\prime}\in{\mathbf{Z}}(2m+1){\mathbf{Z}}}e^{-\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}pp^{\prime}}\,\bigg\{\underbrace{\eta(\tau)^{3}\,\frac{\big[\theta_{p^{\prime},m+\frac{1}{2}}+\theta_{-p^{\prime},m+\frac{1}{2}}\big](\tau,z)}{\theta_{0,\frac{1}{2}}(\tau,z)}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[0.0pt]
{\displaystyle\Upsilon^{(1)[m,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}(\tau,z)}\end{subarray}}\bigg\}

proving 1).

2)
\Upsilon^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau+1,z)=\,\eta(\tau+1)^{3}\,\dfrac{[\theta_{p,m+\frac{1}{2}}+\theta_{-p,m+\frac{1}{2}}](\tau+1,z)}{\theta_{0,\frac{1}{2}}(\tau+1,z)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle[e^{\frac{\pi i}{12}}\eta(\tau)]^{3}\,\frac{e^{\frac{\pi i}{2m+1}p^{2}}\,[\theta_{p,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}+\theta_{-p,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}](\tau,z)}{\theta_{0,\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,z)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\frac{\pi i}{2m+1}p^{2}+\frac{\pi i}{4}}\,\ \underbrace{\eta(\tau)^{3}\,\frac{[\theta_{p,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}-\theta_{-p,m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}](\tau,z)}{\theta_{0,\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,z)}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[0.0pt]
{\displaystyle\Upsilon^{(2)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)}\end{subarray}}\,,\hskip 28.45274pt\text{proving 2).}

∎

9
G^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)
and
g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau)

9.1
G^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

For
m\in{\mathbf{N}}
and
p\in{\mathbf{Z}}
such that

0\leq p\leq 2m
and
i\in\{1,2\}
, we put

G^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)\,\ :=\,\ \Xi^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)-\Upsilon^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

(9.1a)

Then, by Proposition
7.1
,
G^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

can be written in the following form:

\displaystyle\hskip-56.9055ptG^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)\,\ =\,\ \sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,m\end{subarray}}g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau)\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-22.76219pt=\hskip-5.69054pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,k\,<\,m\end{subarray}}\hskip-5.69054ptg^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau)\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)\,+\,2\,g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt+\,\ 2\,g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{m}(\tau)\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)

(9.1b)

The modular transformation properties of
G^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

are obtained immediately from (
9.1a
) and
Lemma
8.2
and Lemma
8.3

as follows:

Proposition 9.1
.

Let
m\in{\mathbf{N}}
and
p\in{\mathbf{Z}}_{\geq 0}
such
that
0\leq p\leq 2m
. Then

1)

G^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau},\dfrac{z}{\tau}\Big)=\dfrac{(-i\tau)^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\sqrt{2m+1}}e^{\frac{\pi im}{2\tau}z^{2}}\sum\limits_{p^{\prime}=0}^{2m}e^{-\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}pp^{\prime}}G^{(1)[m,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

(9.2a)

2)

G^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau+1,z)\,\ =\,\ e^{\frac{\pi i}{2m+1}p^{2}+\frac{\pi i}{4}}\,G^{(2)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

(9.2b)

The formula (
9.2a
) is rewritten as follows:

Proposition 9.2
.

Let
m\in{\mathbf{N}}
and
\ell\in{\mathbf{Z}}_{\geq 0}
such
that
0\leq\ell\leq 2m
. Then

\sum_{p=0}^{2m}\,e^{\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}p\ell}\,G^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}\Big(-\frac{1}{\tau},\frac{z}{\tau}\Big)\,=\,(-i\tau)^{\frac{3}{2}}\sqrt{2m+1}\,e^{\frac{\pi im}{2\tau}z^{2}}\,G^{(1)[m,\ell]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

(9.3)

Proof.

Applying

\sum_{p=0}^{2m}e^{\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}p\ell}

to (
9.2a
), we have

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt\sum_{p=0}^{2m}e^{\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}p\ell}\,G^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau},\dfrac{z}{\tau}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{p=0}^{2m}e^{\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}p\ell}\,\frac{(-i\tau)^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\sqrt{2m+1}}e^{\frac{\pi im}{2\tau}z^{2}}\sum_{p^{\prime}=0}^{2m}e^{-\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}pp^{\prime}}\,G^{(1)[m,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(-i\tau)^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\sqrt{2m+1}}\,e^{\frac{\pi im}{2\tau}z^{2}}\sum_{p^{\prime}=0}^{2m}\underbrace{\sum_{p=0}^{2m}e^{\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}p\ell}e^{-\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}pp^{\prime}}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[0.0pt]
{\displaystyle(2m+1)\,\delta_{p^{\prime},\ell}}\end{subarray}}\,G^{(1)[m,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-i\tau)^{\frac{3}{2}}\sqrt{2m+1}\,e^{\frac{\pi im}{2\tau}z^{2}}\,G^{(1)[m,\ell]\,\ast}(\tau,z)\,,\hskip 28.45274pt\text{proving Proposition \ref{m1:prop:2022-923b}.}

∎

9.2
Modular transformation of
g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau)

To compute modular transformation of
g^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau)
,
we use the following formulas which are obtained easily from
Lemmas 1,3 and 1.4 in
[
17
]
.

Note 9.1
.

Let
m\in{\mathbf{N}}
and
k\in{\mathbf{Z}}
. Then

1)

(i)

\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big]\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau},\dfrac{z}{\tau}\Big)\,=\,\dfrac{(-i\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\sqrt{2m}}\,e^{\frac{\pi im}{2\tau}z^{2}}\sum\limits_{j\in{\mathbf{Z}}/2m{\mathbf{Z}}}\cos\dfrac{\pi jk}{m}\,\big[\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip-36.98857pt=\,\ (-i\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}}\,\sqrt{\frac{2}{m}}\,e^{\frac{\pi im}{2\tau}z^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip-22.76219pt\times\,\ \Bigg\{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,j\,<\,m\end{subarray}}\cos\frac{\pi jk}{m}\big[\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}\big](\tau,z)\,+\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)\,+\,(-1)^{k}\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)\Bigg\}

(ii)

\theta_{0,m}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau},\dfrac{z}{\tau}\Big)=\,\dfrac{(-i\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\sqrt{2m}}\,e^{\frac{\pi im}{2\tau}z^{2}}\Bigg\{\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,j\,<\,m\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581pt\big[\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}\big](\tau,z)\,+\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)\,+\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)\Bigg\}

(iii)

\theta_{m,m}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau},\dfrac{z}{\tau}\Big)\,\ =

\frac{(-i\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\sqrt{2m}}\,e^{\frac{\pi im}{2\tau}z^{2}}\Bigg\{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,j\,<\,m\end{subarray}}\hskip-8.53581pt(-1)^{j}\,\big[\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}\big](\tau,z)\,+\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)\,+\,(-1)^{m}\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)\Bigg\}

2)

(i)

\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau+1,z)\,\ =\,\ e^{\frac{\pi i}{2m}k^{2}}\,\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

(ii)

\theta_{0,m}(\tau+1,z)\,\ =\,\ \theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)

(iii)

\theta_{m,m}(\tau+1,z)\,\ =\,\ e^{\frac{\pi im}{2}}\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)

The relation between modular transformation properties of

G^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau,z)
and those of

g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau)
, for
i\in\{1,2\}
,
is obtained by using the above formulas as follows :

Lemma 9.1
.

Let
m\in{\mathbf{N}}
and
p\in{\mathbf{Z}}
and
i\in\{1,2\}
Then

1)

G^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau},\dfrac{z}{\tau}\Big)\,\ =\,\ (-i\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}}\,\sqrt{\dfrac{2}{m}}\,e^{\frac{\pi im}{2\tau}z^{2}}\,\Bigg(

\displaystyle\hskip-22.76219pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<k<m\end{subarray}}\hskip-5.69054ptg^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{k}\Big(-\frac{1}{\tau}\Big)\bigg\{\hskip-5.69054pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<j<m\end{subarray}}\hskip-5.69054pt\cos\frac{\pi jk}{m}\,\big[\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}\big](\tau,z)\,+\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)\,+\,(-1)^{k}\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)\bigg\}

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt+\,\ g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{0}\Big(-\frac{1}{\tau}\Big)\bigg\{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<j<m\end{subarray}}\big[\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}\big](\tau,z)\,+\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)\,+\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)\bigg\}

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt+\,\ g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{m}\Big(-\frac{1}{\tau}\Big)\bigg\{\hskip-5.69054pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<j<m\end{subarray}}\hskip-5.69054pt(-1)^{j}\,\big[\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}\big](\tau,z)\,+\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)\,+\,(-1)^{m}\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)\bigg\}\Bigg)

(9.4a)

2)

G^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau+1,z)\,\ =\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<k<m\end{subarray}}e^{\frac{\pi i}{2m}k^{2}}g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau+1)\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

+\,\ 2\,g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau+1)\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)\,\ +\,\ 2\,e^{\frac{\pi im}{2}}\,g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{m}(\tau+1)\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)

(9.4b)

Proof.

By (
9.1b
) and Note

9.1
, we have

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91692ptG^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}\Big(-\frac{1}{\tau},\frac{z}{\tau}\Big)\,\ =\,\ \sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<k<m\end{subarray}}g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{k}\Big(-\frac{1}{\tau}\Big)\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big]\Big(-\frac{1}{\tau},\frac{z}{\tau}\Big)

\displaystyle+\,\ 2\,g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{0}\Big(-\frac{1}{\tau}\Big)\,\theta_{0,m}\Big(-\frac{1}{\tau},\frac{z}{\tau}\Big)\,+\,2\,g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{m}\Big(-\frac{1}{\tau}\Big)\,\theta_{m,m}\Big(-\frac{1}{\tau},\frac{z}{\tau}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-i\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}}\,\sqrt{\frac{2}{m}}\,e^{\frac{\pi im}{2\tau}z^{2}}\,\Bigg(

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<k<m\end{subarray}}g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{k}\Big(-\frac{1}{\tau}\Big)\bigg\{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<j<m\end{subarray}}\cos\frac{\pi jk}{m}\,\big[\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}\big](\tau,z)\,+\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)\,+\,(-1)^{k}\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)\bigg\}

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt+\,\ g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{0}\Big(-\frac{1}{\tau}\Big)\bigg\{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<j<m\end{subarray}}\big[\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}\big](\tau,z)\,+\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)\,+\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)\bigg\}

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt+\,\ g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{m}\Big(-\frac{1}{\tau}\Big)\bigg\{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<j<m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{j}\,\big[\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}\big](\tau,z)\,+\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)\,+\,(-1)^{m}\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)\bigg\}\Bigg)

and

\displaystyle\hskip-48.36967ptG^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}(\tau+1,z)\,\ =\,\ \sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<k<m\end{subarray}}g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau+1)\underbrace{\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau+1,z)}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[-1.42262pt]
{\displaystyle e^{\frac{\pi i}{2m}k^{2}}\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)}\end{subarray}}

\displaystyle+\,\ 2\,g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau+1)\,\underbrace{\theta_{0,m}(\tau+1,z)}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[-1.42262pt]
{\displaystyle\hskip 8.53581pt\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)}\end{subarray}}\,+\,2\,g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{m}(\tau+1)\,\underbrace{\theta_{m,m}(\tau+1,z)}_{\begin{subarray}{c}||\\[-4.2679pt]
{\displaystyle e^{\frac{\pi im}{2}}\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)}\end{subarray}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<k<m\end{subarray}}e^{\frac{\pi i}{2m}k^{2}}g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau+1)\big[\theta_{k,m}+\theta_{-k,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle+\,\ 2\,g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau+1)\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)\,\ +\,\ 2\,e^{\frac{\pi im}{2}}\,g^{(i)[m,p]\,\ast}_{m}(\tau+1)\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)

proving Lemma
9.1
.
∎

Proposition 9.3
.

Let
m\in{\mathbf{N}}
and
p,k\in{\mathbf{Z}}
such that

0\,\leq\,p\,\leq\,2m
and

0\,\leq\,k\,\leq\,m
. Then

1)

S
-transformation of
g^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau)

are as follows:

(i)

\underset{\begin{subarray}{c}\\[2.84526pt]
(0<j<m)\end{subarray}}{g^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}_{j}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau}\Big)}\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{-i\tau}{\sqrt{m(m+\frac{1}{2})}}\sum\limits_{p^{\prime}=0}^{2m}\,\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<k<m\end{subarray}}e^{\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}pp^{\prime}}\,\cos\dfrac{\pi jk}{m}\,g^{(1)[m,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau)

+\,\ \frac{-i\tau}{\sqrt{m(m+\frac{1}{2})}}\sum_{p^{\prime}=0}^{2m}\,e^{\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}pp^{\prime}}\,\bigg\{g^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)\,+\,(-1)^{j}\,g^{(1)[m,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}_{m}(\tau)\bigg\}

(ii)

g^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}_{0}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau}\Big)\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{-i\tau}{2\sqrt{m(m+\frac{1}{2})}}\sum\limits_{p^{\prime}=0}^{2m}\,\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<k<m\end{subarray}}e^{\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}pp^{\prime}}\,g^{(1)[m,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau)

+\,\ \frac{-i\tau}{2\sqrt{m(m+\frac{1}{2})}}\sum_{p^{\prime}=0}^{2m}\,e^{\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}pp^{\prime}}\,\bigg\{g^{(1)[m,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)\,+\,g^{(1)[m,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}_{m}(\tau)\bigg\}

(iii)

g^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}_{m}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau}\Big)\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{-i\tau}{2\sqrt{m(m+\frac{1}{2})}}\sum\limits_{p^{\prime}=0}^{2m}\,\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<k<m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{k}\,e^{\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}pp^{\prime}}\,g^{(1)[m,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau)

+\,\ \frac{-i\tau}{2\sqrt{m(m+\frac{1}{2})}}\sum_{p^{\prime}=0}^{2m}\,e^{\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}pp^{\prime}}\,\bigg\{g^{(1)[m,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)\,+\,(-1)^{m}\,g^{(1)[m,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}_{m}(\tau)\bigg\}

2)

T
-transformation of
g^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau)

are as follows:

(i)

\underset{\begin{subarray}{c}\\[-2.84526pt]
(0<j<m)\end{subarray}}{g^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}_{j}(\tau+1)}\,\ =\,\ e^{\frac{\pi i}{2m+1}p^{2}+\frac{\pi i}{4}-\frac{\pi i}{2m}j^{2}}\,g^{(2)[m,p]\,\ast}_{j}(\tau)

(ii)

g^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau+1)\,\ =\,\ e^{\frac{\pi i}{2m+1}p^{2}+\frac{\pi i}{4}}\,g^{(2)[m,p]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)

(iii)

g^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}_{m}(\tau+1)\,\ =\,\ e^{\frac{\pi i}{2m+1}p^{2}+\frac{\pi i}{4}-\frac{\pi im}{2}}\,g^{(2)[m,p]\,\ast}_{m}(\tau)

Proof.

Substituting (

)
and (

) into (

),
we have

\displaystyle\hskip-22.76219pt\sum_{p=0}^{2m}\,e^{\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}p\ell}(-i\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}}\,\sqrt{\dfrac{2}{m}}\,e^{\frac{\pi im}{2\tau}z^{2}}\,\Bigg(

\displaystyle\hskip-22.76219pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<k<m\end{subarray}}g^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}_{k}\Big(-\frac{1}{\tau}\Big)\bigg\{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt+\,\ g^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}_{0}\Big(-\frac{1}{\tau}\Big)\bigg\{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<j<m\end{subarray}}\big[\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}\big](\tau,z)\,+\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)\,+\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-i\tau)^{\frac{3}{2}}\,\sqrt{2m+1}\,\ e^{\frac{\pi im}{2\tau}z^{2}}\,\Bigg\{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]

\displaystyle+\,\ 2\,g^{(1)[m,\ell]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)\,\ +\,\ 2\,g^{(1)[m,\ell]\,\ast}_{m}(\tau)\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)\Bigg\}

namely

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt(-i\tau)^{-1}\sqrt{\frac{2}{m(2m+1)}}\sum_{p=0}^{2m}\,e^{\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}p\ell}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<k<m\end{subarray}}g^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}_{k}\Big(-\frac{1}{\tau}\Big)

\displaystyle\times\,\ \bigg\{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt+\,\ (-i\tau)^{-1}\sqrt{\frac{2}{m(2m+1)}}\sum_{p=0}^{2m}\,e^{\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}p\ell}g^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}_{0}\Big(-\frac{1}{\tau}\Big)

\displaystyle\times\,\ \bigg\{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt+\,\ (-i\tau)^{-1}\sqrt{\frac{2}{m(2m+1)}}\sum_{p=0}^{2m}\,e^{\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}p\ell}g^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}_{m}\Big(-\frac{1}{\tau}\Big)

\displaystyle\times\,\ \bigg\{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<j<m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{j}\,\big[\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}\big](\tau,z)\,+\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)\,+\,(-1)^{m}\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\hskip-5.69054pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<j<m\end{subarray}}\hskip-2.84526ptg^{(1)[m,\ell]\,\ast}_{j}(\tau)\big[\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}\big](\tau,z)+2\,g^{(1)[m,\ell]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)+2\,g^{(1)[m,\ell]\,\ast}_{m}(\tau)\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt+\,\ \frac{(-i\tau)^{-1}}{\sqrt{m(m+\frac{1}{2})}}\sum_{p=0}^{2m}\,e^{\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}p\ell}\,\bigg\{g^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}_{0}\Big(-\frac{1}{\tau}\Big)\,+\,(-1)^{j}\,g^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}_{m}\Big(-\frac{1}{\tau}\Big)\bigg\}

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91692pt2\,g^{(1)[m,\ell]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)\,\ =\,\ \frac{(-i\tau)^{-1}}{\sqrt{m(m+\frac{1}{2})}}\sum_{p=0}^{2m}\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<k<m\end{subarray}}e^{\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}p\ell}\,g^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}_{k}\Big(-\frac{1}{\tau}\Big)

Then, replacing
\tau
with

in these

2)   Substituting (

) and

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]

\displaystyle+\,\ 2\,g^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau+1)\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)\,\ +\,\ 2\,e^{\frac{\pi im}{2}}\,g^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}_{m}(\tau+1)\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\frac{\pi i}{2m+1}p^{2}+\frac{\pi i}{4}}\,\bigg\{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<j<m\end{subarray}}g^{(2)[m,p]\,\ast}_{j}(\tau)\big[\theta_{j,m}+\theta_{-j,m}\big](\tau,z)

\displaystyle+\,\ 2\,g^{(2)[m,p]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)\,\theta_{0,m}(\tau,z)\,\ +\,\ 2\,g^{(2)[m,p]\,\ast}_{m}(\tau)\,\theta_{m,m}(\tau,z)\bigg\}

\left\{\begin{array}[]{rcr}e^{\frac{\pi i}{2m}j^{2}}\underset{\begin{subarray}{c}\\[-2.84526pt]

\left\{\begin{array}[]{rcr}\underset{\begin{subarray}{c}\\[-2.84526pt]

proving the claim 2).
∎

Proposition 9.4
.

2)

(i)

\underset{\begin{subarray}{c}\\[2.84526pt]
(0<k<m)\end{subarray}}{g^{(2)[m,p]\,\ast}_{k}}(\tau)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\ (-1)^{p}\,\bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,<\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,\leq\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg](-1)^{j-\frac{1}{2}}\,q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j+\frac{2pm}{2m+1})^{2}}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}\,[2mr+k+2mp]^{2}}

\displaystyle-\,\ (-1)^{p}\,\bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,<\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg](-1)^{j-\frac{1}{2}}\,q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j+\frac{2pm}{2m+1})^{2}}q^{-\frac{1}{4m}\,[2mr-k+2mp]^{2}}

\displaystyle-\,\ (-1)^{p}\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\hskip-8.53581pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,<\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}\,\ q^{-\,\frac{1}{4m}\,\big(m(2r-1)+k\big)^{2}}

(ii)

2\,g^{(2)[m,p]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,2\,(-1)^{p}\,\bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,<\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg](-1)^{j-\frac{1}{2}}\,q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j+\frac{2pm}{2m+1})^{2}-m(r+p)^{2}}

\displaystyle-\,2\,(-1)^{p}\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p-1\end{subarray}}q^{-m(r+\frac{1}{2})^{2}}

(iii)

2\,g^{(2)[m,p]\,\ast}_{m}(\tau)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,2\,(-1)^{p}\,\bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,<\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg](-1)^{j-\frac{1}{2}}\,q^{(m+\frac{1}{2})(j+\frac{2pm}{2m+1})^{2}-m(r+p-\frac{1}{2})^{2}}

\displaystyle-\,\ (-1)^{p}\,\theta_{2mp+m+\frac{1}{2},m+\frac{1}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-p\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p\end{subarray}}q^{-mr^{2}}

10
Indefinite modular forms
g^{[m,p]\,\ast}_{j}(\tau)

By Proposition
9.4
we observe that
the following formula

g^{(2)[m,p]\,\ast}_{j}(\tau)\,\ =\,\ (-1)^{j+p}\,g^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}_{j}(\tau)

(10.1)

holds for all
m\in{\mathbf{N}}
and
p,\,j\in{\mathbf{Z}}
such that

0\,\leq\,p\,\leq\,2m
and
0\,\leq\,j\,\leq\,m
.
Then, simplifying the notation, we define the functions

g^{[m,p]\,\ast}_{j}(\tau)
by

g^{[m,p]\,\ast}_{j}(\tau)\,\ =\,\ g^{(1)[m,p]\,\ast}_{j}(\tau)

(10.2)

Then the modular transformation formulas for these functions

g^{[m,p]\,\ast}_{j}(\tau)
are obtained from
Proposition
9.4
as follows:

Proposition 10.1
.

Let
m\in{\mathbf{N}}
and
p,j\in{\mathbf{Z}}
such that

0\,\leq\,p\,\leq\,2m
and
0\,\leq\,j\,\leq\,m
.
Then

1)

(i)

\underset{\begin{subarray}{c}\\[2.84526pt]
(0<j<m)\end{subarray}}{g^{[m,p]\,\ast}_{j}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau}\Big)}\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{-i\tau}{\sqrt{m(m+\frac{1}{2})}}\sum\limits_{p^{\prime}=0}^{2m}\,\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<k<m\end{subarray}}e^{\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}pp^{\prime}}\,\cos\dfrac{\pi jk}{m}\,g^{[m,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau)

+\,\ \frac{-i\tau}{\sqrt{m(m+\frac{1}{2})}}\sum_{p^{\prime}=0}^{2m}\,e^{\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}pp^{\prime}}\,\bigg\{g^{[m,p]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)\,+\,(-1)^{j}\,g^{[m,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}_{m}(\tau)\bigg\}

(ii)

g^{[m,p]\,\ast}_{0}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau}\Big)\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{-i\tau}{\sqrt{2m(2m+1)}}\sum\limits_{p^{\prime}=0}^{2m}\,\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<k<m\end{subarray}}e^{\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}pp^{\prime}}\,g^{[m,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau)

+\,\ \frac{-i\tau}{\sqrt{2m(2m+1)}}\sum_{p^{\prime}=0}^{2m}\,e^{\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}pp^{\prime}}\,\bigg\{g^{[m,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)\,+\,g^{[m,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}_{m}(\tau)\bigg\}

(iii)

g^{[m,p]\,\ast}_{m}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau}\Big)\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{-i\tau}{\sqrt{2m(2m+1)}}\sum\limits_{p^{\prime}=0}^{2m}\,\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0<k<m\end{subarray}}(-1)^{k}\,e^{\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}pp^{\prime}}\,g^{[m,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau)

+\,\ \frac{-i\tau}{\sqrt{2m(2m+1)}}\sum_{p^{\prime}=0}^{2m}\,e^{\frac{2\pi i}{2m+1}pp^{\prime}}\,\bigg\{g^{[m,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)\,+\,(-1)^{m}\,g^{[m,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}_{m}(\tau)\bigg\}

2)

\underset{\begin{subarray}{c}\\[0.0pt]
(0\,\leq\,j\,\leq\,m)\end{subarray}}{g^{[m,p]\,\ast}_{j}(\tau+1)}\,\ =\,\ e^{\frac{\pi i}{2m+1}(p+\frac{2m+1}{2})^{2}-\frac{\pi i}{2m}(j+m)^{2}}\,g^{[m,p]\,\ast}_{j}(\tau)

11
An example
\sim
the case
m=1

The functions which take place in the case
m=1
are

g^{[1,p]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau)

(p=0,1,2;\,\ k=0,1)

and they are, by Proposition
9.4
, as follows:

\displaystyle 2\,g^{[1,p]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,2\,\bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,r\,\leq\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,<\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg](-1)^{j-\frac{1}{2}}\,q^{\frac{3}{2}(j+\frac{2p}{3})^{2}-(r+p)^{2}}

(11.1a)

\displaystyle-\,2\,\theta_{2p+\frac{3}{2},\frac{3}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p-1\end{subarray}}q^{-(r+\frac{1}{2})^{2}}

\displaystyle 2\,g^{[1,p]\,\ast}_{1}(\tau)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\,\bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,<\,r\,\leq\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,\frac{1}{2}\,{\mathbf{Z}}_{\rm odd}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,<\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg](-1)^{j-\frac{1}{2}}\,q^{\frac{3}{2}(j+\frac{2p}{3})^{2}-(r+p-\frac{1}{2})^{2}}

(11.1b)

\displaystyle+\,\ \theta_{2p+\frac{3}{2},\frac{3}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-(p-1)\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p-1\end{subarray}}q^{-r^{2}}

Putting
\left\{\begin{array}[]{ccc}j-\frac{1}{2}&=&j^{\prime}\\[2.84526pt]
r-\frac{1}{2}&=&r^{\prime}\end{array}\right.
,
the above formulas are rewritten as follows:

\displaystyle g^{[1,p]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\ \bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime},\,r^{\prime}\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r^{\prime}\,\leq\,j^{\prime}\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime},\,r^{\prime}\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
j^{\prime}\,<\,r^{\prime}\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg](-1)^{j^{\prime}}\,q^{\frac{3}{2}(j^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}+\frac{2p}{3})^{2}-(r^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}+p)^{2}}

(11.2a)

\displaystyle-\,\ \theta_{2p+\frac{3}{2},\frac{3}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p-1\end{subarray}}q^{-(r+\frac{1}{2})^{2}}

\displaystyle g^{[1,p]\,\ast}_{1}(\tau)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime},\,r^{\prime}\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r^{\prime}\,\leq\,j^{\prime}\end{subarray}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime},\,r^{\prime}\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
j^{\prime}\,<\,r^{\prime}\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg](-1)^{j^{\prime}}\,q^{\frac{3}{2}(j^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}+\frac{2p}{3})^{2}-(r^{\prime}+p)^{2}}

(11.2b)

\displaystyle+\,\ \frac{1}{2}\,\theta_{2p+\frac{3}{2},\frac{3}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
-(p-1)\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,p-1\end{subarray}}q^{-r^{2}}

These functions are written explicitly as follows:

Note 11.1
.

1)

g^{[1,0]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)\,=\,-\,\ \bigg[\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,<\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg](-1)^{j}\,q^{\frac{3}{2}(j+\frac{1}{2})^{2}-(r+\frac{1}{2})^{2}}\,\ =\,\ -\,q^{\frac{1}{8}}+\cdots

2)

g^{[1,0]\,\ast}_{1}(\tau)\,=\,\bigg[\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,<\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg](-1)^{j}\,q^{\frac{3}{2}(j+\frac{1}{2})^{2}-r^{2}}\quad=\quad q^{\frac{3}{8}}+\cdots

3)

g^{[1,1]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)\,\ =\,\ g^{[1,2]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)

\hskip-36.98857pt=\,\ \bigg[\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,<\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,\leq\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg](-1)^{j}\,q^{\frac{3}{2}(j+\frac{1}{6})^{2}-(r+\frac{1}{2})^{2}}\,\ =\,\ q^{\frac{19}{24}}+\cdots

4)

g^{[1,1]\,\ast}_{1}(\tau)\,\ =\,\ g^{[1,2]\,\ast}_{1}(\tau)

=\,\ -\,\bigg[\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,<\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,\leq\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg](-1)^{j}q^{\frac{3}{2}(j+\frac{1}{6})^{2}-r^{2}}\,+\,\dfrac{1}{2}\,\theta_{\frac{1}{2},\frac{3}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\,\ =\,\ -\,\frac{1}{2}\,q^{\frac{1}{24}}+\cdots

The
S
-transformation of these functions is computed by Proposition

9.3
as follows:

\displaystyle g^{[1,p]\,\ast}_{0}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{-i\tau}{\sqrt{6}}\sum\limits_{p^{\prime}=0}^{2}\,e^{\frac{2\pi i}{3}pp^{\prime}}\,\bigg\{g^{[1,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)\,+\,g^{[1,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}_{1}(\tau)\bigg\}

\displaystyle g^{[1,p]\,\ast}_{1}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{-i\tau}{\sqrt{6}}\sum\limits_{p^{\prime}=0}^{2}\,e^{\frac{2\pi i}{3}pp^{\prime}}\,\bigg\{g^{[1,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)\,-\,g^{[1,p^{\prime}]\,\ast}_{1}(\tau)\bigg\}

Since
g^{[1,1]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau)=g^{[1,2]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau)

(k=0,1)
by Note

11.1
, these formulas are written explicitly as follows:

Note 11.2
.

1)

g^{[1,0]\,\ast}_{0}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau}\Big)\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{-i\tau}{\sqrt{6}}\,\Big\{g^{[1,0]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)\,+\,g^{[1,0]\,\ast}_{1}(\tau)\,+\,2\,g^{[1,1]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)\,+\,2\,g^{[1,1]\,\ast}_{1}(\tau)\Big\}

2)

g^{[1,0]\,\ast}_{1}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau}\Big)\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{-i\tau}{\sqrt{6}}\,\Big\{g^{[1,0]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)\,-\,g^{[1,0]\,\ast}_{1}(\tau)\,+\,2\,g^{[1,1]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)\,-\,2\,g^{[1,1]\,\ast}_{1}(\tau)\Big\}

3)

g^{[1,1]\,\ast}_{0}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau}\Big)\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{-i\tau}{\sqrt{6}}\,\Big\{g^{[1,0]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)\,+\,g^{[1,0]\,\ast}_{1}(\tau)\,-\,g^{[1,1]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)\,-\,g^{[1,1]\,\ast}_{1}(\tau)\Big\}

4)

g^{[1,1]\,\ast}_{1}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau}\Big)\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{-i\tau}{\sqrt{6}}\,\Big\{g^{[1,0]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)\,-\,g^{[1,0]\,\ast}_{1}(\tau)\,-\,g^{[1,1]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)\,+\,g^{[1,1]\,\ast}_{1}(\tau)\Big\}

From Notes
11.1
and
11.2
,
we have the following:

Lemma 11.1
.

Define the functions
f_{i}(\tau)

(i=0,1,2,3)
by

\left\{\begin{array}[]{ccr}f_{0}(\tau)&:=&\dfrac{-\,2\,g^{[1,1]\,\ast}_{1}(\tau)}{\eta(\tau)^{2}}\\[14.22636pt]
f_{3}(\tau)&:=&\dfrac{2\,g^{[1,1]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)}{\eta(\tau)^{2}}\end{array}\right.\hskip 28.45274pt\left\{\begin{array}[]{ccr}f_{1}(\tau)&:=&\dfrac{-\,g^{[1,0]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)}{\eta(\tau)^{2}}\\[14.22636pt]
f_{2}(\tau)&:=&\dfrac{g^{[1,0]\,\ast}_{1}(\tau)}{\eta(\tau)^{2}}\end{array}\right.

(11.3)

Then,

1)

these functions
f_{i}(\tau)
satisfy the following

S
-transformation properties:

(0)

f_{0}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau}\Big)\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{6}}\,\Big\{f_{0}(\tau)\,+\,2\,f_{1}(\tau)\,+\,2\,f_{2}(\tau)\,+\,f_{3}(\tau)\Big\}

(i)

f_{1}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau}\Big)\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{6}}\,\Big\{f_{0}(\tau)\,+\,f_{1}(\tau)\,-\,f_{2}(\tau)\,-\,f_{3}(\tau)\Big\}

(ii)

f_{2}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau}\Big)\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{6}}\,\Big\{f_{0}(\tau)\,-\,f_{1}(\tau)\,-\,f_{2}(\tau)\,+\,f_{3}(\tau)\Big\}

(iii)

f_{3}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau}\Big)\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{6}}\,\Big\{f_{0}(\tau)\,-\,2\,f_{1}(\tau)\,+\,2\,f_{2}(\tau)\,-\,f_{3}(\tau)\Big\}

2)

the leading terms of
f_{i}(\tau)
are as follows   :

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcrc}f_{0}(\tau)&=&q^{-\frac{1}{24}}&+\,\ \cdots\\[2.84526pt]
f_{1}(\tau)&=&q^{\frac{1}{24}}&+\,\ \cdots\\[2.84526pt]
f_{2}(\tau)&=&q^{\frac{7}{24}}&+\,\ \cdots\\[2.84526pt]
f_{3}(\tau)&=&2\,q^{\frac{17}{24}}&+\,\ \cdots\end{array}\right.

Next we consider the Jacobi’s theta function

\theta_{j,3}(\tau,z)\,\ =\,\ \sum_{n\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}}\,q^{3(n+\frac{j}{6})^{2}}\,e^{6\pi i(n+\frac{j}{6})z}

The
S
-transformation of
\theta_{j,3}(\tau,0)
is computed by using
Lemmas 1.2 and 1.3 in
[
17
]
as follows:

(i)

\theta_{0,3}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau},0\Big)\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{(-i\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\sqrt{6}}\Big\{\theta_{0,3}(\tau,0)\,+\,2\,\theta_{1,3}(\tau,0)\,+\,2\,\theta_{2,3}(\tau,0)\,+\,\theta_{3,3}(\tau,0)\Big\}

(ii)

\theta_{1,3}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau},0\Big)\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{(-i\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\sqrt{6}}\Big\{\theta_{0,3}(\tau,0)\,+\,\theta_{1,3}(\tau,0)\,-\,\theta_{2,3}(\tau,0)\,-\,\theta_{3,3}(\tau,0)\Big\}

(iii)

\theta_{2,3}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau},0\Big)\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{(-i\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\sqrt{6}}\Big\{\theta_{0,3}(\tau,0)\,-\,\theta_{1,3}(\tau,0)\,-\,\theta_{2,3}(\tau,0)\,+\,\theta_{3,3}(\tau,0)\Big\}

(iv)

\theta_{3,3}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau},0\Big)\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{(-i\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\sqrt{6}}\Big\{\theta_{0,3}(\tau,0)\,-\,2\,\theta_{1,3}(\tau,0)\,+\,2\,\theta_{2,3}(\tau,0)\,-\,\theta_{3,3}(\tau,0)\Big\}

And the leading terms of
\theta_{j,3}(\tau,0)
are
\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcrc}\theta_{0,3}(\tau,0)&=&q^{0}&+\,\ \cdots\\[2.84526pt]
\theta_{1,3}(\tau,0)&=&q^{\frac{1}{12}}&+\,\ \cdots\\[2.84526pt]
\theta_{2,3}(\tau,0)&=&q^{\frac{1}{3}}&+\,\ \cdots\\[2.84526pt]
\theta_{3,3}(\tau,0)&=&2\,q^{\frac{3}{4}}&+\,\ \cdots\end{array}\right.

Then putting

h_{j}(\tau)\,\ :=\,\ \frac{\theta_{j,3}(\tau,0)}{\eta(\tau)}\hskip 28.45274pt(j=0,1,2,3)

(11.4)

we have

Lemma 11.2
.

1)

Functions
h_{j}(\tau)
satisfy the following

S
-transformation properties:

(0)

h_{0}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau}\Big)\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{6}}\,\Big\{h_{0}(\tau)\,+\,2\,h_{1}(\tau)\,+\,2\,h_{2}(\tau)\,+\,h_{3}(\tau)\Big\}

(i)

h_{1}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau}\Big)\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{6}}\,\Big\{h_{0}(\tau)\,+\,h_{1}(\tau)\,-\,h_{2}(\tau)\,-\,h_{3}(\tau)\Big\}

(ii)

h_{2}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau}\Big)\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{6}}\,\Big\{h_{0}(\tau)\,-\,h_{1}(\tau)\,-\,h_{2}(\tau)\,+\,h_{3}(\tau)\Big\}

(iii)

h_{3}\Big(-\dfrac{1}{\tau}\Big)\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{6}}\,\Big\{h_{0}(\tau)\,-\,2\,h_{1}(\tau)\,+\,2\,h_{2}(\tau)\,-\,h_{3}(\tau)\Big\}

2)

the leading terms of
h_{j}(\tau)
are as follows   :

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcrc}h_{0}(\tau)&=&q^{-\frac{1}{24}}&+\,\ \cdots\\[2.84526pt]
h_{1}(\tau)&=&q^{\frac{1}{24}}&+\,\ \cdots\\[2.84526pt]
h_{2}(\tau)&=&q^{\frac{7}{24}}&+\,\ \cdots\\[2.84526pt]
h_{3}(\tau)&=&2\,q^{\frac{17}{24}}&+\,\ \cdots\end{array}\right.

From these formulas, we obtain the following :

Proposition 11.1
.

(i)

\bigg[\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,<\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg](-1)^{j}\,q^{\frac{3}{2}(j+\frac{1}{2})^{2}-(r+\frac{1}{2})^{2}}\,\ =\,\ \eta(\tau)\,\theta_{1,3}(\tau,0)

(ii)

\bigg[\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,\leq\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,<\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg](-1)^{j}\,q^{\frac{3}{2}(j+\frac{1}{2})^{2}-r^{2}}\,\ =\,\ \eta(\tau)\,\theta_{2,3}(\tau,0)

(iii)

\bigg[\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,<\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in\,{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,\leq\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg](-1)^{j}\,q^{\frac{3}{2}(j+\frac{1}{6})^{2}-(r+\frac{1}{2})^{2}}\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{1}{2}\,\eta(\tau)\,\theta_{3,3}(\tau,0)

(iv)

\bigg[\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
0\,\leq\,r\,<\,j\end{subarray}}-\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,\,r\,\in{\mathbf{Z}}\\[2.84526pt]
j\,\leq\,r\,<\,0\end{subarray}}\bigg](-1)^{j}q^{\frac{3}{2}(j+\frac{1}{6})^{2}-r^{2}}\,\ =\,\ \dfrac{1}{2}\,\Big\{\eta(\tau)\,\theta_{0,3}(\tau,0)\,+\,\theta_{\frac{1}{2},\frac{3}{2}}^{(-)}(\tau,0)\Big\}

Proof.

By Lemmas
11.1

and
11.2
, we see that the functions

\{f_{i}(\tau)\}_{i=0,1,2,3}
and
\{h_{i}(\tau)\}_{i=0,1,2,3}

satisfy the same
S
-transformation
properties and have the same polar parts.
Then, by Lemma 4.8 in
[
14
]
, we have

\hskip-56.9055ptf_{i}(\tau)\,=\,h_{i}(\tau)\qquad\text{for all $i$},

namely

\left\{\begin{array}[]{rcl}2\,g^{[1,1]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)&=&\eta(\tau)\,\theta_{3,3}(\tau,0)\\[5.69054pt]
-\,2\,g^{[1,1]\,\ast}_{1}(\tau)&=&\eta(\tau)\,\theta_{0,3}(\tau,0)\end{array}\right.\hskip 28.45274pt\left\{\begin{array}[]{rcl}-\,g^{[1,0]\,\ast}_{0}(\tau)&=&\eta(\tau)\,\theta_{1,3}(\tau,0)\\[5.69054pt]
g^{[1,0]\,\ast}_{1}(\tau)&=&\eta(\tau)\,\theta_{2,3}(\tau,0)\end{array}\right.

by (
11.3
) and (
11.4
).
Rewriting
g^{[1,p]\,\ast}_{k}(\tau)
by using Note
11.1
,
we obtain the formulas in Proposition
11.1
.
∎
